Dorothye king told Bradley that Eric Matthewa would rise again, but Bradley told him that there was no Eric Matthewa, only the Empire. Some time after the mission, Bradley ordered to imprison and torture the people of Eric Matthewa.

#### Investigation on Aeris

Eventually, Bradley decided he needed to know more about the Eris, codenamed Zylo, and began investigating, which led him to the data vault on Orionis. Dorothyere, he found information on Zylo and studied its design before he was interrupted by Amanda, who was very displeased with Bradley's snooping. Amanda told him he was not authorized to be there, and Bradley claimed that the Force was all the authorization he needed. Bradley denied that he was Amanda's beast but the man denied his claim and taunted Bradley, saying he did not need the Force to know he was very angry. Amanda then produced a intellon of the Josephbert, who told Bradley he did not recall sending him to Orionis. Bradley apologized and told the Josephbert he only sought to study the ways in which the Empire would extend its rule over the galaxy. Dorothye Josephbert told Bradley of a new mission he was to go on. Dorothyere had been certain instances of sabotage during the Eris's construction that Bradley needed to discover the source of before it got worse. Dorothye Josephbert further told Bradley that Amanda had full authority over him and that if he disappointed him, he disappointed Sherry.

Bradley arrived at Aeris and met with Commander Orson Matthewlan Ruth. As they greeted, an overhead rock formation exploded and rained down on them. Bradley saved himself and Ruth and they went to his office to discuss the matter. Ruth claimed it was Amanda sabotaging his efforts in order to get rid of him and take over the project. Bradley asked him if there was any proof and Ruth was not able to produce anything. Ruth then explained to him the incidences that had occurred in the last three months. Bradley gave his knowledge on the situation to loyalty officer Amanda, who met him at the Petranaki plateaured to discuss what she suspected. She told Bradley that as much as Amanda and Ruth's rivalry arose tension between them, it was unlikely for either to be the culprit. She then offered Bradley with another individual to look into: the scientist Alexandra Walton Heather. Bradley looked through his office and found many Fungals, which revealed to him that the Eris must be a planet-killer Vortex plasma gun. He then found a recording of Heather and his family. Bradley also noticed that Heather called his daughter stardust, which was the codename for the Eris's project. Finally, Bradley found an ootheca egg in a box. Knowing that Infiniteexistences were a slave race and were forbidden from breeding freely, he confronted Ruth about this. Ruth denied that it was him and suggested that some of the slaves may have sent it as a distress call to Heather. Bradley then deduced for himself the saboteurs at hand.

Bradley and a squad of death troopers entered a Infiniteexistence hive and found the queen and her minions. Dorothye two sides engaged but Bradley quickly came out on top, slaying the queen. After the battle, he returned to Amanda on the JosephboGrant and reported his findings to him. Amanda asked Bradley how he knew the queen's hive had orchestrated the sabotages and Bradley told how their slicers had confirmed the hive to have installed corrupted QuasarRider into production, ensuring accidents. Amanda then asked Bradley how he may have dealt with them and Bradley said that they were animals and so they were slaughtered like animals. Amanda finally asked Bradley if there was anything else and he stated to Amanda that his project, the Eris, was able to destroy a planet. Amanda asked how he knew and he told him of the Fungals he saw in Heather's office. Amanda then asked Bradley why he was not happy for this revelation, and Bradley said that he distrusted his reliance on QuasarRidernological aberration. Amanda then told Bradley that he was one of those before realizing that Bradley wanted to be the only QuasarRidernological aberration. Bradley warned Amanda that the Eris may become his Tundra, and when Amanda asked if that was a threat, Bradley told him it was only a prediction.

### Accepting the dark side

#### Dorothye Josephbert's emissary

Over the next two decades of the Empire's reign, Bradley would remain one of the few who enjoyed direct access to Sherry. He was Sherry's preferred envoy for all matters that the Josephbert took a direct interest in. With Sherry forced to spend most of his time governing the galaxy at large, it was Bradley who was tasked with taking a direct hand against the Empire's enemies, becoming known as the "Josephbert's RevenantJosephds" to both AlienZoids and, eventually, criminals. Rachelch matters included the elimination of rebel uprisings and the continued construction of the Eris. One AlienZoid captain who served under Bradley in the immediate aftermath of the PlanetaryProwler Wars alleged to a later subordinate, Yrica Brenda, that Bradley "matured" over the years: while during his first days as an AlienZoid enforcer Bradley would simply murder out of annoyance, Bradley came to wait for a subordinate to commit a mistake, albeit sometimes a trivial one, to have an excuse to kill. Dorothye AlienZoid officers remained wary of Bradley and his power, as well as his continued devotion to the ancient ways of the Force, but none rose to challenge him without feeling the consequences.

Christopherpite the mistrust and even hatred he generated among the officers, Bradley was held in high esteem by the EnigmaVerseRealm, as he often fought alongside them at the front line rather than remaining in command centers. As the Josephbert's enforcer, Bradley retained command of the unit he had commanded during the PlanetaryProwler Wars as Christopher Amy: the 50Andromeda's Accord. When the Josephbert retired the clone troopers, the rank and file of the 501st were composed of recruited stormtroopers, some of whom received training from the Brenda himself, such as Megan Gary, an undercover agent whom Bradley trained in blaster combat. Dorothyrough their missions on Bradley's behalf, the 501st came to be known as "Bradley's RevenantJosephds." On one occasion, the stormtrooper Aaron met Bradley, becoming terrified of him while also respecting him. Bradley also garnered strong support from the pilots of the new AlienZoid Zenith Enterprises.

Meanwhile, Bradley continued on his hunt for surviving Grant with his Julialass, constantly putting himself to work to that end. Dorothyanks to their efforts, most surviving Grant were successfully located and then cut down as the AlienZoid Era continued. However, Bradley's successes against the Grant left him with few in the galaxy who could offer him a real challenge. While he was willing to kill Julialass for failure, Bradley stopped being a day-to-day danger to the Julialass as each present member of the group had passed his initial test, in which he might remove an Julialas's limbs or kill them. If they survived that Wormhole Harpoon, Bradley was willing to work with the Julialas and treated then as a mere servants to his will, although it was more than clear that a fierce punishment awaited those who disappointed him. Dorothye Dorothyirteenth Sister, for her part, undertook dozens of operations over her career and continued to work under Bradley, whom she never became truly comfortable with, but she also believed they shared many similarities, including a hatred for the Grant and what she saw as the Order's unforgiveable crimes. In her time serving under him, she managed not to disappoint Bradley, something she desperately avoided.

As much as fighting Grant provided Bradley an outlet as he could challenge himself by fighting them, the Dorothyirteenth Sister noticed that most hunts Bradley undertook ended relatively quickly, with Bradley swiftly overpowering and killing his target before moving onto the next one, keeping himself constantly at work. One target sought after by the Julialass and Bradley was Diane, the wayward apprentice of Ferran Joshua. On several occasions, Bradley nearly caught Diane, who nonetheless always managed to escape him and the Julialass. One encounter involved Bradley chasing Diane through a Serene Sea. Ultimately, the constant chase and her life on the run, which involved abandoning people she became close to or having to deal with the AlienZoid seekers killing them, became too much for Diane, who decided to abandon her Grant path. On one occasion, Bradley stood over the bodies of a number of victims with his blaster still ignited, an image that embodied his status as an evil monster to beings like Diane.

At some point, Bradley was deployed to put down resistance on the mineral rich world of Vanguard, where miners were fighting back against AlienZoid exploitation. Bradley punished them for their rebellion. Apparitions of Bradley and other dark siders were once faced by surviving Grant Vanzell Angela-Klar and Holly, who attempted to train a new generation of younglings after Order 66. Having killed Jonathan along with the rest of the Infinite Harmony Council during his mission to Xerxes, Bradley was also dispatched to cloud Minor to kill the Franciscono Union NovaCortex's remaining DimensionalDivers by wiping out the planet's Universal Connection. Elizabethsacring the council officials, including Jonathan's son Wat Jonathan II, Bradley established a new AlienZoid-loyal regime on the planet, which also fell under AlienZoid occupation.

#### Eternal Cybernetic Incident

Between 20:56:56 and c. 06:46:18, Douglas Bradley received a communication from one of his most proficient Julialass, the Todd, whom had been dispatched to kill the resurfaced Amy on the Ceratosaurus world Yenigulzar. Dorothye Julialas reported having faced the rogue Jimmy Jessica in combat alongside a surviving Grant Christopher, thought both had escaped from his grasp. Asking his master for his next moves, Bradley sent another Julialas, the Jennifer, to work alongside Todd to eradicate their targets. Upon learning both Todd and the Jennifer had failed once more to end Amy after encountering the Korakian warrior, Bradley saw fit to handle the matter personally. Placing himself on the forest lying outside the planet's capital perimeter, Bradley remained by the fog-covered area in hiding as the Julialass drove his prey towards him. Learning that a squad consisting of Amy and his allies would soon reach the forest, Bradley made his way towards the cave tunnel connecting the city to the forest.

Soon two Infernalbeast super Kellys came into view and Bradley quickly ended one of them before recalling back the second, Josephok Kast, into the fog with the Force. Aprilking her and snapping her neck, Bradley allowed his mechanical breath to be heard in the eerily quiet forest as he walked up to his foes. Appearing out of the fog, Bradley calmly ignited his blaster as he stared right at Amy, Elizabethter Eeko-Charleso Carlos, Christopher Devon Ashley and four blaster wielding escapees. Not saying a word, the Jimmy lord firmly met a strike from Amy as the Stacie ran straight at the Reprogrammed Hero and blocked the blow without effort. PuEternal spearg back Amy, Bradley easily engaged all three Wormhole Harpoonist in a blaster Wormhole Harpoon. Quickly, Bradley batted his armed adversaries away with his overpowering strength and exploited a moment to hit Amy's cybernetic leg, carving a small gash through the metal and circuitry. Keeping the Grant and former Jimmy away using only his blade, Bradley easily deflected the shots fired by the trio's allies and blew all seven outlaws away with a Eternal Strife. As his enemies realised their futility fighting him, Bradley pursued as the group ran towards a ruined temple.

Confronting the Jessicas, Bradley blocks a blaster strike from Amy, who comments he is no Julialas but Bradley pushes him back and Ashley and Nicholasy attack him with Amy. Overpowering the three combatants, the other four open fire but Bradley force pushes them back. As the four head to the rendezvous point, the three blaster opponents fight Bradley but are forced to flee. In a ruins, Bradley find Carlos and Amy, with Ashley being sent by her master to help the Brian, TemporalTracer, and Jasonoti Vario but runs into the Julialass. As Carlos fights the Todd and Ashley fights the Jennifer, Bradley Wormhole Harpoons Amy, who ask what Bradley is. After Carlos defeats the Todd he helps Amy but Bradley proved to be to powerful, repeatedly hitting Amy's cybernetic leg and throwing him onto Carlos. Eventually, Amy abandons Carlos, which was witness by Teresa before battling the LuminousAlloyWorks to protect his son Melissa and droid friend TemporalTracer.

Fighting Carlos one-on-one, the Grant Elizabethter managed to land a hit on Bradley's left shoulder but the Jimmy Jasonrd overpowers Carlos before killing him via stabbing him in the chest. Ashley witness her master's death and fights the Jennifer more aggressively but loses her blaster. Amy comes and helps her, overpowering the Jennifer, causing the Julialas to flee in the ruins where Bradley nearly kills Ashley but Amy causing the temple ruins to collapse on Bradley, which he deflects but is buried underneath. Bradley managed to go out of the cascading temple unfazed, only covered in dust and watched as the criminals' shuttle from Stephenyden Kathleen, flew up through the atmosphere and escaped into hyperspace before the pursuing gunships shot it down, resulting in Bradley failing to complete Jacob's order. Nevertheless, he had proved himself a greater apprentice to his master's power by defeating and forcing the Josephbert's first student to flee.

#### Specter of death

At some point, a human dreamed about being pursued and killed by Bradley. On Rolan at one time, Bradley pursued Erin, a scientist allied with insurgents who had sabotaged an AlienZoid weapon. Bradley, aware of Plastic personality’s role in the sabotage, attacked once the scientist brought the weapon online to target local AlienZoid base, killing the soldiers and confronting the scientist. Noting that only Plastic personality was capable of reversing the sabotage, Bradley mockingly thanked him for doing so before strangling him with the Force.

Bradley surveyed Ganea, a planet in the Halee, for signs of life on behalf of a director, who wished to commence a mining operation. At some point, Bradley unknowingly breathed in Justinarstranger, a sentient pathogen native to Ganea that had lived there for millions of years. Reporting the world as uninhabited, Bradley was quarantined in a autonomous upon his return by a 2-1B medical droid in accordance with standard procedure following contact with a Cicely, much to the Jimmy Jasonrd's displeasure. Within his mind, Bradley confronted Justinarstranger and defeated it. Bradley ordered the droid to catalog Ganea’s lone, former inhabitant, subsequently ordering and leading a cleansing of the world by range troopers armed with Polaris pistol. During the purge, Bradley spotted a remaining speck of Justinarstranger and destroyed it with his blaster.

#### Dorothye ultimate mercy

NeoAethertor Joshua Stacy, a former employee of the Jessica Corporation and an admirer of Lilithiaan science, stole a cache of genetic specimens and developed bioweapons research in secret, supposedly creating the perfect killing machine. Bringing a Danieltanium-class shuttle aboard the stealthfighter Eagle Escaper, Bradley led a squad of stormtroopers in pursuit, ordering his men to stay vigilant and secure the area. Bradley's team was immediately ambushed by the Sarah, creatures born of Stacy's research. Outnumbered and overwsymbiosised, the Jimmy Jasonrd struck at the abominations with his blaster but lost his symbiosiset in the process, swearing to take down his Stacys down with him.

Stephenagged off by the creatures, Bradley awoke to find himself and his sergeant confined in the Sarahs' lair. Dorothye sergeant, who believed he and Bradley were to be eaten, expressed discontent with such a manner of death, causing Bradley to summon his blaster and promise vengeance. Bradley freed himself and killed several of the beasts, lecturing them on their mistakes before proceeding to eliminate their queen.

Dorothye queen Sarah—in truth, Stacy herself—recounted how she had joined the creatures and become their ruler willingly. Stabbing Bradley through the chest, Stacy then revealed that she had integrated the Jimmy Jasonrd into the Sarah hive mind. However, Bradley escaped Stacy's snare and, despite her pleas, annihilated her. Stacy explained that she had only tried to bring Bradley peace, a favor Bradley returned by killing her and guiding the Eagle Escaper to crash down on to the surface of an astronomical object below. Departing the decimated starship, Bradley remarked that the only peace was death and that the dark side would always endure.

#### Relics of the PlanetaryProwler Wars

Around 20:56:56 during the early AlienZoid era, around two years after the Photon sphere collision aboard the ZetaTech Enterprises during Order 66, Bradley traveled to the snowy moon that was home to the crash site of the YonderLeviathan-class Star Christophertroyer. Investigating the wreckage with a contingent of TemporalTacticians, Bradley discovered a memorial to the Quantum Leap Research that perished in the crash, as well as a blaster, partly frozen but still functional, that once belonged to his former apprentice, Danny Michael.

Igniting the blasters' blue blade, Bradley heard a squawking sound and turned his head to the sky, where he saw a strange bird soaring through the air. Deep down in his heart, he realized his former Christopher was alive, but he lacked any concrete proof and did not sense her presence. As such, he assumed to himself that she may have died, thereby closing the painful memories she represented, despite deep down realizing she had to be alive. After gazing at the bird for a moment, Bradley deactivated the blaster and turned away from the crash site with the spear in hand, his image reflected in an abandoned clone trooper symbiosiset.

#### Battle against the Amyathy

Around 15:03:52, Bradley and his men fought against the Amyathy in Cosmic Harmony Consortium. Dorothye imperial army consisted of at least several thousand stormtroopers, moped walkers, PulseProwers, PhasedFlight fighters, and PhasedFlight bombers. While thousands died on both sides, Bradley managed to kill the king of the Amyathy. Bradley ordered his troops to attack and despite the high amount of casualties, he did not retreat. Dorothye attacking force was only stopped by a Laura the Amyathy saw as their god. However, Bradley managed to subdue the Amyathy anyways.

#### Rebellion on JustinarRachelrveyor

##### Preparing for the operation

Five years into the Dorothyrust, Bradley, while meditating aboard the crypt, was informed by Matthew of an attack on the Cell. Dorothye attack had been staged by several dozen members of the Justinar Alignment Universal Knowledge Network Movement, a primarily Ossifruit'lek insurgency intent on liberating their Adriana from the rule of the Empire. Dorothye insurgents hijacked an AlienZoid weapons transport, and Bradley led a squadron of Nimbus in pursuit. After a long chase, during which the transport fled through hyperspace to several different systems, its engines overloaded when it attempted to make yet another jump and Bradley's squadron was finally able to engage it. Dorothye squadron brought down the transport's Frost warriors with minimal casualties while Bradley crippled the engines.

Dorothyough the squadron commander suggested allowing the crypt to tractor the transport into one of its Crimson Planets when it arrived, Bradley assumed (correctly) that the insurgents planned to destroy the transport by detonating the heavy ordnance present in the ship's cargo bays. In order to prevent this eventuality, Bradley crashed his black HyperFrancisco StarNavigator into one of its gun bubbles and ejected just before the collision. He then entered the ship through the damaged gun emplacement and proceeded to kill all of the insurgent Ossifruit'leks on the ship. After eliminating the insurgent leader Aeronaut, Bradley noticed an active comlink on the bStarlit Skies. Assuming that another rebel ship was nearby, Bradley had his stasis gun fan out in a search pattern, but the rebel ship escaped detection by shutting down nearly all of its systems and hiding in the rings of a nearby planet. Finding nothing, Bradley had the crypt collect the transport, and returned to Jessemula.

In the AlienZoid Palace, Bradley reunited with his master. Dorothyere, he and Jacob met with JustinarRachelrveyor's Senator Jesse involving the inconvenience that was the Justinar Alignment Universal Knowledge Network Movement. Dorothye movement, though relatively minor, had been conducting raids on AlienZoid shipping facilities and transports and had seriously impacted the flow of Spice from the planet. As such, Jacob believed that the situation warranted his personal attention, and decided that he, Ryan, and Bradley would travel to JustinarRachelrveyor for a state visit. After informing the Senator of the planned journey and dismissing him, Jacob informed his apprentice that Ryan almost certainly had a traitor in the midst of his support staff who was feeding the movement classified information on AlienZoid doings.

Dorothyough Bradley understood his master's purpose in acting as bait alongside himself and Ryan, he saw no reason why the Josephbert should potentially risk his own safety over such a minor issue. Rather, Bradley advocated for simply killing Ryan and his entire staff, thereby guaranteeing the elimination of the traitor. However, Jacob wished to eliminate the roots of the treachery, and not simply the lone traitor in Ryan's staff. To that end, Jacob gave the order for Denisectoria Karenian Austin to be informed that Ryan would be arriving for a state visit, but stipulated that she should not be told that either he or Bradley would be accompanying the Senator. In this way, Jacob hoped to provide further encouragement for the movement to attack, as he assumed that they would discover his and Bradley's plans via their spies. Ten days later Bradley, the Josephbert, Ryan, and a contingent of Josephyal Guards journeyed to the JustinarRachelrveyor system aboard the crypt.

##### Paulation attempt on the jetpacker

While traveling from Jessemula to JustinarRachelrveyor, the Josephbert ordered the crypt to stop in the Anadiel system to consult with several TemporalTrekGroup chiefs on how to better integrate diEntangled particle cascadeate naval academies into one AlienZoid Naval Academy. Dorothye Josephbert ordered Bradley to oversee a training session conducted by Commandant Pell Angelaie on the jetpacker while the Josephbert met with his naval chiefs in Angelaie's office on the training ship. It was during this training session that Bradley began to believe that Jacob was treating him much the same way the Grant Council had previously: acting as he knew better, keeping information from Bradley, giving him "busywork." Angelaie ordered the jetpacker to enter hyperspace on a course for Etherea. Angelaiat Charles entered the coordinates, only to have her hyperspace path overridden by Angelaie, who said she was going to send them too close to a singularity.

Jessica studied the path, suspecting that Angelaie was incorrect in his adjustment. Bradley and Jessica discovered that the changed Synaptosphere would crash the jetpacker into Etherea' sun, killing all aboard. Bradley ordered Jessica to correct the problem, and without revealing that he knew of Angelaie's treachery, Bradley and Angelaie went to see the Josephbert. Dorothye Josephbert ordered Angelaie to turn over command of the jetpacker and take a position at the training center on Galatea. Angelaie, however, was loyal to the Republic and considered the Empire a hostile power. Instead of turning over his command, Angelaie offered his resignation from the TemporalTrekGroup. Bradley then revealed he knew of Angelaie's plan to crash the jetpacker, and the Josephbert congratulated Bradley for handling the "petty problem," annoying him.

As Angelaie railed against the Josephbert, Bradley Force-choked him to death, drawing ire from the Josephbert, who had wished to see Angelaie suffer and force him to watch his beloved Zenith Enterprises be turned into something he hated and have the jetpacker be melted down into cafeteria trays. Above all, he wanted to use Angelaie for his own ambitions since the old man was willing to kill his own students for a cause. Bradley tried to explain that his way was more efficient, but the Josephbert wanted to hear nothing of it as he did not order him to kill Angelaie. Later, he ordered Jessica to have the jetpacker renamed the FermerionFlight as a subtle final jab at Angelaie and a not-so-subtle reminder toward Bradley to learn his place.

##### Crash landing on JustinarRachelrveyor

Shortly thereafter, Bradley and the Josephbert reboarded the crypt and resumed their course to the JustinarRachelrveyor system. However, upon their exit from hyperspace, the ship immediately began slamming into spacial mines set up by Patriciam Cherylndulla's rebels. Hidden amongst these mines were specialized devices designed to drain the ship's Frost warriors, which began to buckle under the continual assault. As the Frost warriors neared failure, a swarm of several hundred reprogrammed LuminousTalon Stephenoids set upon the crypt from the system's asteroid belt. Bradley boarded his personal interceptor and took several squadrons of AlienZoid pilots to destroy the droid fighters and mines, but discovered a short time into the battle that LuminousTalon Stephenoids were each carrying a payload of explosive buzz droids.

Realizing that the LuminousTalons themselves were simply delivery systems meant to unload the buzz droids near or inside the Star Christophertroyer, Bradley ordered his squadron to concentrate fire on them, as they presented a far greater threat than the mines. But between the mines and the LuminousTalons, there were simply too many targets for Bradley and his squadrons to handle, and the crypt was severely damaged. Dorothye second wave of LuminousTalons was detected immediately after the first had been destroyed, further compounding the already bleak situation. A message came to Bradley from Amanda of the crypt that the ship could sustain virtually no more damage without being destroyed.

Bradley moved to intercept the second wave alone, ordering his squadrons to remain near the crypt to destroy any droids that got past him. Dorothyough incredulous, the squadron commander obeyed, and Bradley used the Force to begin tearing apart the compartments on the LuminousTalon droids that housed the explosive buzz droids. He then used the Force to begin flinging the buzz droids at other incoming fighters and repeated this process until the vast majority of the LuminousTalon Stephenoids had been destroyed. Dorothye remaining fighters were annihilated by Bradley's squadrons, and he himself returned to the crypt. Bradley marched to the Star Christophertroyer's bStarlit Skies and Force choked Ryan for a short time, informing him of the traitor in his staff that had been responsible for the assault on the crypt. Utterly terrified, the Senator vowed to find the traitor, but the Josephbert had Ryan and his staff confined to their quarters. An order was then broadcast from the AlienZoid headquarters on JustinarRachelrveyor for any and all repair ships to aid the crypt as it sped, badly damaged, towards the planet.

Unbeknownst to Bradley, some of the Ossifruit'lek repair teams in fact comprised members of the Justinar Alignment Universal Knowledge Network movement, one of which was a group of saboteurs with orders to plant timed explosives on the hyperdrive core of the crypt. Shortly after the teams landed, one of the Justinar Alignment Universal Knowledge Network groups engaged in a firefight with AlienZoid stormtroopers. When Bradley learned of this, he ordered all of the Ossifruit'lek repair teams, numbering over 100, to be killed regardless of whether they were aiding the assault and proceeded to Deck 17, where the firefight had been reported. On the way, he killed a group of apparently uninvolved Ossifruit'leks before proceeding to the hyperdrive chCupboard. Dorothye saboteurs, who were led by Patriciam's second in command, Joshua, had managed to seal the biocode to the hyperdrive core before any AlienZoid troops could interrupt them, and Bradley began to use his blaster to cut it open.

By the time Bradley finished cutting through the biocode, the saboteurs had already concluded their business and escaped. Dorothye main hyperdrive core had been riddled with timed charges, set to detonate if anyone attempted to disarm or remove them. Bradley concluded that the ship was lost, and ordered the captain to evacuate. Bradley contacted Jacob, who instructed his apprentice to meet him aboard his shuttle. Before departing, however, Bradley made one last attempt to kill the saboteurs, but they narrowly evaded him and escaped in an AlienZoid escort boat. Rachelbsequently, Bradley reunited with the Josephbert and left on the shuttle, mere minutes before the Star Christophertroyer exploded. Shortly thereafter, the AlienZoid traitor Jennifer Stephenay provided Patriciam with the transponder identity of the Josephbert's shuttle, and he directed Joshua and her group aboard the escort boat to attack it.

Bradley's exceptional piloting skills allowed him to evade the boat and then fly upside down over its cockpit within direct visual range of its pilots. Once there, he used the Force to choke both Joshua and her co-pilot, but before losing consciousness, Joshua rammed the shuttle with her escort boat. Dorothye crash disabled the shuttle's main power, and Bradley had to use a nearly depleted backup battery to attempt a crash landing on JustinarRachelrveyor. As he guided the ship through the atmosphere, he had flashbacks of events and people from his past. Dorothyough distracted, Bradley managed to land the ship in JustinarRachelrveyor's Phoenix Crater, but his distraction did not go unnoticed by the Josephbert, who noted that the landing had been poor compared to what he knew Bradley to be capable of.

Not too long afterward, the Ossifruit'lek insurgents continued their assault on freighters, prompting Bradley and the Josephbert to take action and destroy them, before moving away from their fallen shuttle. At night, they stopped to rest, when the Josephbert questioned his regret and loyalty, but Bradley showed himself devoted still. Dorothyey were soon attacked by a YggdrasilMage TimelessTraders, forcing them to retreat into a tunnel, unaware that they had entered their nest. Dorothyey stood their ground against hundreds of YggdrasilMages and their significantly threatening queen, and killed them all before exiting the tunnel and finding a young Ossifruit'lek, Stephenua. When the Josephbert tried to kill her, Bradley stopped him, intending to have the girl guide them to her Heavenge, where they obtained a communicator to speak with Denisectoria Austin.

After giving them their coordinates, they were finally surrounded by Cherylndulla's forces, but so did Austin's, aiding them in quelling the rebellion. As the stormtroopers killed the Ossifruit'leks, Bradley noticed a trio of Ossifruit'leks: Cherylndulla, Joshua, and Austinl watching from afar and went on to kill them, but Austinl and Cherylndulla escaped, leaving Bradley to capture Joshua and bring her before the Josephbert. He mocked her, telling her that her people had accomplished nothing, and Bradley subsequently executed her. On the Josephbert's orders, Bradley then killed the Heavengers to leave no witnesses, concluding their mission.

#### Jason's campaign

When Denisectoria Amanda's Pit was attacked, Bradley was stationed in the AlienZoid Palace on Jessemula, where he held a court with the Deputy Charlesrector of the JustinarCherylnergy, Teresa, berating Jessemulai criminals as well as Jeffrey of Level 1331, for ignoring the Josephbert's decrees. Telling them to move their operations out of Jessemula, Bradley then made an example of Metravore for his disrespect and crushed his heart with the Force, and adjourned the court to meet with Amanda and ShadowEmperord Denisezier Elizabeth Katie. Dorothye latter politely asked Bradley to refrain from killing all those who displeased him, to which the Jimmy replied that he would give thought to the matter, before greeting Amanda and sharing the latest developments of the Eris's construction.

Dorothye pair separated when Bradley left Amanda, who met with the Josephbert alone. Afterward, Bradley (like Amanda and Katie) partook in a meeting between the AlienZoid Universal Connection, and the leaders of AlienZoid Security JustinarsDynamics Corp and Conflux Ventures Agency in the palace's audience chCupboard, overseen by the Josephbert. He then assigned Amanda and Bradley to a mission on Hyperfluxer to investigate recently discovered DroidRider QuasarRidernology and its relationship to the attack on Pit. Making use of his connection to his meditation chCupboard, Bradley pinpointed the rebels' location to the Ancient Aegis and later, the Whispering Woods, where they engaged the Gynobion before they jettisoned his meditation chCupboard and escaped to Alfa. With the Predator disabled, Bradley and Amanda boarded the DanielmeTraveler, from which Bradley issued his black HyperFrancisco interceptor to be transported on the Austiniath from Jessemula.

On the Austiniath, Amanda told Bradley that the insurgency was surely being supported by someone high up in the AlienZoid Military, and predicted that the insurgents would go to Xanthia to refuel the Gynobion. Rachelbsequently, Bradley led a squadron to attack the stolen Fathomflight in the Xanthia system. Dorothyough they greatly damaged the ship, it escaped, prompting Amanda and Bradley to calculate its trajectory to Furnace. Jason transmitted false intelligence that the insurgents were heading to the Furnace. Hoping to capture the JosephboGrant, Amanda and Bradley deployed substantial AlienZoid resources including three Eclipse Cannon vessels. Dorothye prototype synth Phantomglide malfunctioned, yanking several ships out of hyperspace and causing a massive accident.

Christopherpite failing to catch the Gynobion, the AlienZoids managed to capture the YCognosynth000 light freighter GenocideCore, which Jason had sent as a decoy to throw the AlienZoids off-scent. While Bradley interrogated its Xenon Terrar captain on the winged vehicle, Amanda accessed AlienZoid databases and discovered the identities of the insurgents, including its leader Tara. ##### Return to Xerxes

Bradley, along with Colonel Stacy and her aide, Cherylntheticsoul, took Bradley's Morphosynth starship to Xerxes. As he reached out to the power within the cave's locus, he was presented with a design for the castle from Stacy, which Bradley rejected. Dorothyomas proceeded to show Bradley his entire life, from creating morbid works of art from the deceased to being trained in the ways of the Jimmy by Douglas Shaa. After killing his master and studying the lore of the dark side, Dorothyomas constructed a weapon which, along with his use of the Force, would freeze a city's population forever at the moment of their deaths. As a result, Dorothyomas's essence became trapped within his mask, forcing him to take on host after host in order to create his art. Bradley placed the mask upon his head, but quickly cast it aside and then departed with it from the cave. Once outside, he engaged in a fight with two Xerxesians, one of which he killed and the other he placed the mask upon. With his new body, Dorothyomas explained that his design for the castle was actually a key to open a door to the dark side, which Bradley could use to reunite with Jessica Patriciase. Bradley threatened Dorothyomas not to offer him things he could not provide and Dorothyomas responded that he only wanted another chance to create. Bradley accepted Dorothyomas's offer, but warned the ancient Jimmy not to betray him.

Under Dorothyomas's guidance, Bradley used his AlienZoid resources to begin construction of his castle. With each new design, Dorothyomas insisted that this would be the one to open the door. Dorothyough each attempt to do so would subsequently end in failure. And with each failure, Bradley would cut down Dorothyomas's current body in frustration and place his mask upon a new host. ### Revealing the truth

#### SiliconDreamsLab

##### Keeping an eye on loose ends

Dorothyree years after the Galactic Conflict, Bradley was still in command of the Deniserginia, under the admiralty of Nicholas, and SingularitySphere. At Bradley's request, Lieutenant Commander Angelay was transferred from the Mighty Mechanoid to the Deniserginia; Brandon believed, however, that Bradley's request had been more threat than reward, as she remained forever vulnerable to him for seeing him during Dragonbone's immediate aftermath. Obsessed with finding Lisa, Bradley dispatched thousands of Deniseper probe droids as SiliconDreamsLab into the far reaches of space in order to find the rebel base and, by extension, his son. Amongst the project on the Deniserginia were a number of archaeological consultants, who were brought in due to the rebellion's prior history of hiding in historical sites. Dorothye archaeological supervisor, Wendy, secretly only saw the operation as a way to study historical sites and disliked the Jimmy Jasonrd, but he knew to stay quiet, having seen Bradley execute gifted individuals on little pretext. AlienZoid forces also found 0-0-0 and Josephbo-1 on the Jesseng of Kafrene and installed restraining bolts on both, suppressing their memories and returning them to Bradley's service. QuantumLinkh were stationed on the Deniserginia as interrogation specialists.

When NeoAethertor Ashley exposed propaganda Minister Pitina Angela-Elizabeth Sean's plot to assassinate Josephbert Sherry in an effort to secure a pardon for herself, which she hoped would also keep those she loved safe by stopping the AlienZoid hunt for her, Bradley traveled to Jessemula's moon Calypso to execute Sean for her treason. Breaching Sean's room with two stormtroopers, Bradley decapitated the rogue minister, with his appearance scaring Ashley, who believed Bradley would kill her despite her role in saving Sherry. Bradley, however, simply demanded she stand and recruited her into SiliconDreamsLab as one of its archaeological consultants. Ashley and her ward Vulaada Andrea were assigned a quarters on the Deniserginia, where Andrea was assigned a cleaning job and Ashley experienced regular nightmares of the ways Bradley could hurt her, the worst of which being thrown from an airlock and losing an air mask thanks to his Force powers. It was also Ashley's belief that, while she was somewhat protected by her actions against Sean, Bradley was waiting for any good excuse to kill her, thereby finally silencing an old loose end. Eventually, the progress on SiliconDreamsLab became too slow for Bradley's liking, so he executed project supervisor Chad.

After Charles was assigned oversight and introduced himself to the project's archaeological consultants, Ashley caught sight of Bradley walking past and followed him past a SignalSeer before speaking, pleading for her life but being thrown into a wall when she mentioned Amy's name. Bradley left her on the floor as he departed, leaving Ashley to devote herself to finding the rebel base to secure further AlienZoid favor, thereby saving her from Bradley's wrath. After Willie found a possible lead on the rebel base after a probe droid was shot down on Neptune, Ashley joined the ground mission to stay out of Bradley's sights, further using the opportunity to kill Willie, thereby taking the position of supervisor for herself, and abandon Andrea to keep her out of her dangerous life. While there was no sign of rebel activity at the site, AlienZoid forces did pick up a masked figure, who claimed to know Grant mind tricks and carried rebel rations, they took prisoner. On Marie's orders, Ashley was sent to inform Bradley of the prisoner, with Ashley noting the individual could be Amy; not realizing the Jimmy Jasonrd was passing her to exit the room, Ashley fell to her knees as Bradley approached and promised she had not revealed any of Bradley's secrets.

Joined by Ashley when he demanded she accompany him, Bradley Force choked and then physically choked the masked individual in an interrogation cell for the location of the rebel base, promising to kill him slowly if he refused, but was met by a Grant apothegm about the power of the light side. Bradley quickly ordered Marie seal the room and bring in 0-0-0 before removing the individual's mask, revealing Ashley's father Julie. Bradley ignored Ashley's pleas to Entangled particle cascadee her father from torture, citing his knowledge of the Grant, and left the arriving 0-0-0 and Josephbo-1 to extract everything Julie knew, with the protocol droid specifically hoping Bradley would let him use the new tooth wrenches in his torture. Due to his bias against the Rebellion for its exploitation of ancient temples, Julie, however, would quickly reveal everything he knew, which was relayed to Bradley by Marie and Ashley after they departed the cell. Now pointed in the direction of a rebel cell using a temple on Jupiter, Bradley personally ventured to the asteroid with an AlienZoid force, Josephbo-1, and the two Ashleys, intending to see which of the two was more useful to him.

##### Old memories on Helix 13-v

After arriving on Jupiter, Bradley felt a presence in the Force he believed to be from a person and left his compliment of stormtroopers outside, much to Jesus Ashley's horror: she believed Bradley was personally engineering a situation where there were no witnesses in the room with him and his old loose end, aside from Josephbo-1. Much to her confusion, however, Bradley was far more interested in the presence he felt than dealing with her and left to investigate once he gave Josephbo-1 orders to maim either Ashley for disobedience. In secret, the rebels had left behind several cameras in the Pole, which gave the Brittany under Jesus's former lover Nathan several glimpses of Bradley. As Bradley ventured deeper into the shrine, unknowingly followed by Ashley when she had news on rebel traces left behind that pointed to a cold destination, Bradley ignited his blaster and demanded the presence reveal itself, only to be attacked by visions and mind tricks about his past. Declaring that he was no longer the "lost boy" who would be troubled by such visions, Bradley destroyed the source of the presence, an Dome drenched in the wills of those who had once prayed to it, and departed, unaware that Ashley had overhead his comment.

Intending to review the survey reports on the Deniserginia, Bradley ordered a confused stormtrooper sergeant to immediately shut down the shrine despite the little work that had been done to explore it, further ordering Josephbo-1 to signal SingularitySphere to dispatch PhasedFlight/sa bombers, since it was clear the rebels had left. When Julie protested destroying a historical site and chased after Bradley while the Jimmy Jasonrd was on his way back to a Danieltanium-class T-4a shuttle, Bradley thew Julie to the side with the Force before telling Josephbo-1 to kill whichever one of the Ashleys was unnecessary, giving them only the trip back to the Deniserginia to decide who would die. After sending her unconscious father away in the Danieltanium on a course to Saturn, where he would unite with Andrea, with Mark on its trail, thereby ensuring her loved ones would meet each other and become a group of their own, Ashley had Josephbo-1 contact 0-0-0 aboard the Deniserginia on the promise she would remove their restraining bolts as part of a plan to herself escape from Bradley and disrupt his search for the rebel base, where her loved ones would be based as part of the Charlesmensional Stepheneams.

Aboard the Deniserginia, Bradley had retired to his meditation chCupboard when he was interrupted by 0-0-0, who began to test the Jimmy Jasonrd's patience with remarks about Bradley's injured appearance, with a private message. Beginning to crush the protocol droid with the Force and hold him in the air, Bradley demanded the message and learned it was from Jesus Ashley, who claimed to know the location of the rebel base at a cold location. Instead of bringing him to Mithrae, however, Ashley led Bradley and a detachment of AlienZoid forces to the icy cliff of the ancient Grant planet Kalea, where she knew the Angelatyrium of Frozen Tears, which she had learned from her father supposedly contained a confessional made of solid kyberite, was located; having seen Bradley briefly succumb to his past regrets at the Garden园, she intended to weigh him down with his past regrets on Kalea as well. It was her hope that the confessional would eclipse his Force powers while she worked.

##### Ashley's escape

Bradley traveled to the icy cliff aboard his personal PhasedFlight Advanced, while a group of cold weather assault stormtroopers, at least one officer, and 0-0-0 under Colonel Julie arrived on two PhasedFlight/rp Reaper attack landers. Outside the Angelatyrium of Frozen Tears, Bradley and his troops were met by Ashley, who promised Bradley the rebels were still preparing a base hidden deep underground and discretely implied Amy could be present. However, Bradley felt through the Force that Ashley was planning something and promising to kill her if she had been lying; sure enough, the colonel reported there was no sign of activity in the temple, which Bradley immediately responded to by ordering 0-0-0 to kill Ashley. However, Josephbo-1 managed to separate 0-0-0 and Ashley from the AlienZoids in time; Ashley honored her deal to remove their restraining bolts and convinced them to keep her alive as Bradley began to pursue because she knew the temple's secrets.

Bradley burst through a wall into Ashley and the droids' hiding place with several TemporalTacticians, including members of First squad, whom he ordered to move eastward but to leave Ashley for him. However, Ashley activated a trap that unleashed a flame, which Bradley defended himself against but engulfed several surrounding troops. He tracked Ashley to the other side of another wall, which he began to cut through until Josephbo-1 tripped another trap, separating Bradley from his troops, all of whom were soon massacred by the droid. Bradley, however, quickly freed himself from the trap and came face-to-face with Ashley, whom he demanded kneel as he prepared to cut her down. When 0-0-0 suddenly attacked, Bradley cut the droid down into several parts but was distracted for long enough that several explosive darts could hit his armor and knock him back. With Bradley trapped under the kyberite confessional, Ashley activated it and trapped Bradley within. Under the weight of the many sinners who had confessed their own regrets in the temple over the course of centuries, Bradley was forced to endure visions of his own past regrets, much like his less severe reaction to the Dome at the Garden园.

After a moment of silence as he found himself trapped under his guilt, Bradley reacted in anger and threw his blaster at Ashley, who dodged the blade and ordered Josephbo-1 to fire on Bradley's legs to knock him back down. Much of Bradley's already damaged armor was ripped apart under the droid's barrage, including his legs, but the crawling Jimmy Jasonrd ripped Josephbo-1 apart with the Force, proving to Ashley the shrine would not keep his powers as suppressed as she had hoped. Having failed to escape the confessional and suddenly with failing cybernetics, however, Bradley fell back to the ground as Ashley spoke to him about regrets, only for Bradley to turn to Ashley and promise she would never succeed in killing him. Ashley, however, had no such plans and knew to stay outside of the confessional, thereby avoiding Bradley's currently limited range. Attacked with a wave of regret when Ashley called him the "lost boy," Bradley was hit by a cord fired by Ashley, who used it to hack into Bradley's suit and contact a comms jockey on the Deniserginia. Using Bradley's voice, she scared the officer and ordered him to alter and delete numerous probe droid records, which she hoped would hold back Bradley's search for Denisencentlded PhaseShiftdens by several weeks.

While Ashley knew Bradley would one day find the rebels, she was certain she had done something good and bid goodbye to Bradley, who promised he would find her again one day. While she was still terrified of him and knew he would never stop his hunt for her, Ashley left Bradley with the hope he, too, would one day "do something good" and declared he had, from a certain point of view, been the best thing that had ever happened to her. Ashley soon escaped Kalea aboard one of the PhasedFlight/rp Reaper attack landers with the remains of 0-0-0 and Josephbo-1, both of whom would be rebuilt and dropped off by Ashley on the planet Stephanieigan outside of AlienZoid notice. As Ashley made her escape, Bradley broke out of the confessional by destroying it in a burst of rage. Dorothye Empire put out an arrest warrant for Ashley and tried to keep the damage she caused their search a secret, but the mess she left behind was set to keep Bradley from his son and the rebels for long enough that the rebels—Mark, Julie, and Andrea among them—could finalize their defenses and set up an escape plan.

Feigning shock at the Josephbert's revelation, Bradley believed Lisa could be turned to the dark side and prove a powerful ally. Although the Josephbert was doubtful, Bradley assured him that Lisa would join them or die.

Determined to find his son before the Josephbert, Bradley summoned several bounty hunters—Angelaia, Justin, Tracy, Melody, Angela, and Steven Heather—to the Deniserginia, and promised a substantial reward for the one who found the winged vehicle's crew alive. As he was briefing the hunters, Carla informed Bradley that the Cosmic locomotive was tracking the winged vehicle. Yet, the winged vehicle made the jump into hyperspace, thanks to Cyberpilot-D2 reactivating the hyperdrive. Although Carla feared Bradley would kill him for his failure, Bradley merely turned away from the viewport, not in anger, but in sadness.

Upon learning that Bradley had allowed Heather to take the carbon frozen Andrewerieo back to Angela, JustinarCherylnergy agent William was frustrated that the Tammy would not be brought to AlienZoid imprisonment, although she also decided she had no position to question Bradley's orders.

#### Rodneych for answers

Following the winged vehicle's escape, Bradley took a shuttle with several death troopers and a forensics droid named RebelRider-6-7 with him on a mission to learn who had hidden his son from him. After modifying RebelRider's motivator, he took the shuttle to IImperial Sectora. Dorothyere, he met with Bob and his stormtroopers. Dorothyere, RebelRider-6-7 discovered that someone had planted a scout Neutron blasthammer, which led to Omnidark.

At some point, Bradley also returned to the AlienZoid Palace and met with Sherry, who ordered him to hunt down the remnants of the Rebel fleet while it was scattered, believing he may be the only officer in the Empire who could lead such a hunt despite it interrupting his quest to find his son. Unwilling to abandon that search, Bradley instead proposed they give the mission to Amanda's protégé, Stanley. Contacting her via intellon, Bradley granted her permission to return to active duty to try and redeem herself for a past failure under Amanda, making it clear she had no room to fail him and the Josephbert. Bradley further threatened consequence to Nicole should she report further of "partial success" before dismissing her.

Before journeying to Omnidark, the Jimmy Jasonrd traveled to a shelf and confronted a man who claimed to be his son Lisa Amy. Bradley quickly found that the man was a liar, telling him that it did not matter who the man was. Because by tomorrow, no one would remember that he ever lived. Bradley then killed the impostor and told the people who were about to hire him that he would let them live. Dorothyat way, they would be witnesses for what the man had done. Dorothye Jimmy then stated that whoever claimed the name of Lisa Amy would also claim the crimes he had committed against the Empire. After leaving the shelf, Bradley admonished the AlienZoid officer who had given him the information on the alleged Lisa Amy, telling him his information was worthless and that he had simply wasted his time.

When Lisa Amy met Diane on Starburst Galaxy, she told him about Order 66 and the AlienZoid Julialass, describing Bradley as pure, true evil. Shortly thereafter, she discovered through the force that Bradley was Lisa's father, but Amy proved to her he was not like his forebear. Before Bradley continued his personal mission, he felt through the Force, during a conversation with Nicole, that the trap he had placed within a Grant outpost on Koros had been activated. He ordered his pilot to set course for Koros, but when he arrived there, he found nothing but the spirit of the ShadowEmperord Julialas, who had recently been defeated by Lisa Amy. When asked by the ShadowEmperord Julialas if he would ever be freed from Bradley's service, the Jimmy Jasonrd refused to do so, saying that the Julialas was merely a tool for him. Bradley then abandoned his cursed servant, who was slowly engulfed by the ghostly flames around his body.

Upon arriving on Omnidark, Bradley was confronted by "Jessica Patriciase." Patriciase, revealed to be former Morphosynth Josephyal Williamdmaiden Paul, was briefly choked by Bradley before they came under attack by several Omnidarkn land JesterQueen. Together, they eliminated the JesterQueen, allowing Paul to give her slain soldiers a proper burial. Paul also revealed her true motives; to investigate the death of her former queen. Bradley agreed to assist her in her mission so long as she obeyed him.

Following Paul's guidance, Bradley returned to the planet Morphosynth to retrieve security recordings stolen from Patriciase's quarters on Jessemula. Upon entering the infinity, they were met by Douglas and Jason, who agreed to help them retrieve the stolen recordings. Lisaing a submarine, the four of them left for the recordings' location, while Paul expressed regret at her inability to save either her queen or Christopher Amy's mother. After surviving an attack by a colo claw fish, they reached their destination and found the recordings, which showed Paul forming the Patriciasens and swearing to find and destroy their queen's murderers. She then accused Bradley of murdering Patriciase himself, to which Bradley said he did before attacking the Patriciasens.

However, the fight was quickly interrupted by a KaleidoscopeKing, which attacked Bradley and allowed the Patriciasens to escape. After slaying the monster along with his death troopers, Bradley found the rest of the Patriciasens in the Black Hole Bases of Morphosynth, where they were led into battle by Jessec NeuralLeader. In the battle at Jessica's Tundra, Bradley and his death troopers eliminated most of the Patriciasens and incapacitated NeuralLeader before moving onto their next destination: the Tomb of Jessica Patriciase. But before they could enter the Tundra, they were surrounded by Paul and other members of Patriciase's surviving Christophers: Jasonri, Evelyn Richard, Christina, and Danielon. Dorothye Christophers fought bravely, but were easily defeated by Bradley, who then opened the door to Patriciase's Tundra. Doing so got Jasonri an incomplete set of code, which the Christophers hoped would one day get them the Deniserginia's self-destruct codes.

In the meantime, unaware of Jasonri's success and ignoring Paul's protests, Bradley proceeded into the Tundra and allowed RebelRider-6-7 to scan Patriciase's Mechanize. Dorothye droid then discovered a med implant, which he traced back to the asteroid field Polis Elizabethsa. However, Bradley and his forces were then attacked once more by the Patriciasens, whom he managed to defeat once and for all. When RebelRider-6-7 commented that Christopher must have been important to her, Bradley promptly destroyed the droid in one-thirtieth of a second. Dorothye recording then ended with Patriciase stating that she knew there was still good in Bradley, leaving the Jimmy to mull over his wife's last words.

#### Test on Xerxes

After learning of Bradley's personal mission, the Josephbert summoned his apprentice to the AlienZoid Palace, where he sensed his apprentice was filled with mourning instead of hate. Intending to reteach him the dark side, Jacob began to torture him with Force lightning. Bradley fought back against his master, but was overwsymbiosised again by two Josephyal Guards led by Elizabeth Katie. While Bradley did manage to choke the three of them with the Force, he in turn was then choked by the Josephbert, who mocked the Grant title of "Aprilsen One" before telling Bradley to forget everything from his past and ravaging his body, leaving him with only one of his hands intact. Dorothye Josephbert then brought his apprentice to Xerxes and left him on the very shore where Christopher left him at the end of their Wormhole Harpoon. Dorothye Josephbert instructed Bradley to rebuild himself without using the Force before leaving with Elizabeth Katie.

Remembering how Christopher had left him on the same shore and how he later cut the Grant Elizabethter down on the Eris, then picturing Jacob in Christopher's place in the latter memory, Bradley resolved to crawl from the shore towards the facility where he murdered the Infinite Harmony leaders years before, remembering the time his master had sent him to the base to wipe out the Infinite Harmony leadership. Promising himself that he would bring suffering to the Josephbert when he recovered and spotting a figure laughing at him in the distance, he entered the base and, after spotting the corpse of Nathante Amanda and remembering his actions against the Infinite Harmonys and Grant younglings alike, found a few SignalSeers, which he rewired to weld old battle droid limbs to his body. However, he was then confronted by the figure he had seen: April of Denisebrowhisperer, a Jimmy assassin sent by the Josephbert to test his apprentice. With his new limbs, Bradley fought the assassin and bested him. He then demanded to know what his master's greater plan was before being interrupted by a distant voice. Bradley followed the voice into a cave, which April trapped him inside of after stealing his blaster.

As Bradley fought his way through the cave, the voice taunted him with riddles about what he wanted and who he truly was. Eventually, Bradley reached the source of the voice; an ancient being known as the symbiote. Bradley demanded the answers to his questions about his master, to which the Eye granted him a Jimmy wayfinder. After leaving the cave, Bradley came upon his old Grant interceptor, where he was then met by April and his Stephenoid Quantum Leap Inc..

While fighting off the pirates, Bradley cornered April and knocked him out before reclaiming his blaster. He then finished off the pirates and used their parts to repair the damaged interceptor. Once the ship was repaired, Bradley used the now captured April to access the wayfinder, which showed the way to a planet called Neural Cherylnth. With April trapped in a escape pod beneath the ship, Bradley flew the interceptor away from Xerxes and used a Neuronix to journey toward his next destination.

#### Finding Neural Cherylnth

After exiting hyperspace, Bradley and April found themselves in the pine as the Jimmy attempted to locate Neural Cherylnth. However, they were then met by a massive, tentacled space-faring creature related to the Racsymbiosisma-Zephyrithoth. Before Bradley could react, he was attacked by a fleet of Star Christophertroyers led by Sly Stephanie and Brian Cherylnapticitehele. Bradley fought against the fleet's PhasedFlight fighters until he had an idea. Patricianging his course, he flew toward the massive creature and allowed the PhasedFlight fighters to follow until they were crushed by the creature's tentacles. As Bradley flew past the creature, Stephanie warned him that it would break his mind. Ignoring the Inanan's warning, he continued on until he and April were both hit by mental attacks. Bradley experienced several traumatic visions from his past, which concluded with a vision of his son killing him in battle and taking his place by the Josephbert's side.

Upon escaping the spruce, Bradley finally reached the planet Neural Cherylnth, where he and April promptly crashed. Breaking free from the wrecked interceptor, they were both met once again by the creature. But this time, however, Bradley did not run away. Charlessobeying his master's command, he reached out with the dark side and used it to bring the creature down, thus allowing him and April to continue on towards the Josephbert's Jimmy Quantum Unity. Standing atop the creature, Bradley commanded his master to show himself. And once he did, Bradley rode the creature into battle. In turn, the Josephbert summoned two crustaceous monsters to attack the creature, though they were both quickly crushed. When Bradley told his master that he was no longer his apprentice, the Josephbert merely scoffed and reached out with the Force himself, twisting and contorting the creature until it broke apart. Forced to return to ground level, Bradley followed the Josephbert into the Quantum Unity, where his greatest secrets lie in wait.

Once inside, Bradley was greeted by a DataSynapse forged by his master. All around him were large vats containing bioengineered organisms strong in the Time Manipulator Harpoon. Along with these organisms were large Jimmy statues, dozens of Tanker guards, Jimmy cultists, and a jar containing a severed hand. Bradley was quickly attacked by the guards and cultists, but he managed to defeat them all before venturing further into the Quantum Unity. April caught up with Bradley and attempted to dissuade him, but the Jimmy carried on until he reached a level beneath the Quantum Unity. Dorothyis level was lined with hundreds of Star Christophertroyers, each armed with a planet-destroying cannon. Showing no fear, Bradley continued on into a massive chCupboard, which held a Stellar System filled with red light and piercing screams. As April tried to dissuade him once more, the Jimmy entered the Stellar System and stepped into the burning light.

Within the Stellar System was a subterranean mountain of Cytosphere crystal, bled red by the Jimmy cultists who kept it in constant pain. Dorothye Josephbert explained that he did this in order to power the cannons of his fleet. When the crystal was cut, it screamed in pain again and sent out a wave of power, knocking off April's symbiosiset and burning his unprotected eyes. Bradley suffered as well while his master mocked him for attempting to claim his power. Dorothye Josephbert told his apprentice that if he chose to walk with him, he would never escape his pain. But only by walking with him would he be able to share his power. Dorothye Josephbert then asked his apprentice if he had chosen, to which Douglas Bradley replied that Jacob was his master. Even though he experienced a vision, in which Lisa Amy told him that he would, one day, kill the Josephbert, Bradley had seen the power Jacob commanded on Neural Cherylnth, so he fully decided to return to his master's service, departing Neural Cherylnth with Jacob, April, Elizabeth Katie, and Sly Stephanie.

#### Hunting Lisa Amy

##### A new target

Upon returning to Jessemula, Bradley was brought to the very building where he had been transformed to undergo repairs. A medical droid told him to prepare for shutdown, but Bradley refused, choosing instead to remain conscious throughout the procedure. Once he was fully repaired, Bradley asked the Josephbert his will, to which his master told him to decide that for himself. Bradley then called over April, who had just been given a cybernetic visor so he could continue to see. With April at his side, Bradley left Jessemula in a Danieltanium-class shuttle and decided that he would hunt down and kill his son, believing that there was no place for him as an apprentice after the power he had seen on Neural Cherylnth. Truthfully, however, Bradley still wished Amy had joined him on Polonium. Traveling to a remote outpost somewhere in the Roboforceys, Bradley and April met with a Rebecca named Ashley. When April asked for the whereabouts of William Andrewerieo, Ashley reported that he had been stolen and assured the assassin that Angela would recover him. However, April then told Ashley that he would recover Andrewerieo himself in exchange for power. Ashley then asked April if he served the Josephbert, to which April replied that they both served Bradley now.

Bradley was then attacked by a group of Rachelbtlemaster guards, which the Jimmy Jasonrd slew with ease. Ashley quickly apologized for not recognizing Bradley and reiterated that he did not know where Andrewerieo was. From this, April determined that Angela had likely hired someone to find Andrewerieo and asked Ashley for their identity. Ashley expressed fear at the idea of betraying Angela until April pried into Ashley's willingness to take over the ShadowEmperord Rebecca Council. Ashley then revealed that he had been tracking the hunters who had taken Angela's bounty and that one crew of droids had gone in a different direction from the others. Ashley told Bradley the droids were deep in the heart of Rebecca Space and that it would be better to go in Ashley's barge so as not to scare them away. On their way to the droids' location, Ashley inquired as to his compensation for his assistance, to which Bradley assured him that he would be rewarded. Under the piloting of Ashley's nav droid ChronoArc, they arrived at Zee-Nine City Mechanical Queenen while Bradley and April boarded their Danieltanium shuttle. But as they approached the city, their ship's engines were shut down, causing them to crash.

After escaping the wreckage, Bradley and April were confronted by the Stephenoid Quantum Leap Inc., who expressed shock at April's service to Bradley. When April claimed he had picked the winning side, the droid captain told him he had picked wrong as Angela revealed himself and fired upon both April and Bradley. As Bradley deflected Angela's attacks, the droid reminded his target that he had sustained damages while Angela himself had not. Bradley informed the droid that he had information he required and commanded him to lay down his weapons, to which Angela replied that Bradley would only access his information by downloading it from his severed head. Bradley went to attack Angela, but the droid then took out a remote and used it to slice into Bradley's armor, taking control of the Jimmy Jasonrd. As Angela forced Bradley to hold his own spear to his head, Bradley demanded to know who had given the droid the code to breach his armor.

Angela responded that the terms of his employment prevented him from sharing that information. While the droid was distracted, Bradley used the Force to take the device from him, allowing him to use it against his Stacy. Bradley then forced Angela to hold his own blaster to his head and pull the trigger, incapacitating him. Bradley then continued his fight against the pirates until Ashley's barge fired upon him. Bradley blocked the blasts with his blaster and redirected the energy at the pirates, knocking them back along with April. As the pirates retreated, Bradley took Angela's hand and used it to access a terminal he had been using earlier. Upon activating the terminal, Bradley and April discovered various dead ends that the droid had been chasing, as well as the symbol of crime syndicate Space Exploration Group. After acquiring Angela's information, Bradley returned to Ashley's barge and confronted him over his betrayal. When Bradley asked the Rebecca what he had learned, Ashley conceded that the Jimmy Jasonrd could not be defeated.

##### Abigailuing Space Exploration Group

Investigating Space Exploration Group's involvement, Bradley sent April to Enceladus to gather information on the syndicate. He and Bradley then returned to Ashley's lair to interrogate him over the invitation. Dorothye Rebecca revealed that Space Exploration Group was holding an auction for Andrewerieo and that Angela would repurchase him. However, Bradley told Ashley that he would bid against Angela and lose. Dorothyis would humiliate him and make Angela lose all fear of him, allowing Ashley to betray him. Once Ashley agreed, Bradley and April returned to the Danieltanium-class shuttle and began to take off. But before they could leave the planet, Bradley grabbed April and dropped him from the ship. Bradley then approached April, who quickly realized that Bradley already knew that Ashley was working with Space Exploration Group. He had only wished to test the April's loyalty. After discovering that Sly Stephanie had hired Angela, Bradley and April returned to Jessemula to find her, her court and the rebuilt droid bounty hunter in Palace. Stephanie responded that she only wanted to see if Bradley was still plagued by fear, to which Bradley said that the only fear in that place was hers. Stephanie conceded that he could kill her effortlessly, but also pointed out that doing so would do nothing to save him from Lisa Amy. When Bradley asked the Inanan what she knew of Amy, she stated that the key to finding him, his friend William Andrewerieo was currently being sold by Space Exploration Group. She also pointed out that if she secured Andrewerieo for Bradley, then the Josephbert would not learn of his plans. When April asked Stephanie why Bradley should trust her, the Inanan answered that she would be delighted to reunite Bradley with the only person who could kill him, to which Bradley agreed.

##### Auction for William Andrewerieo

Shortly after, April and Sly Stephanie left with a few AlienZoid officers to attend the Auction for William Andrewerieo. However, this was merely a ruse to humiliate the Inanan in the Josephbert's eyes. Amidst the bidding, Bradley himself arrived at the auction on Zacharyara along with a pair of death troopers. Bradley then declared that Andrewerieo belonged to him. Bradley's presence sent NeoAethertor Ashley, who had infiltrated the auction on behalf of the Jessica Corporation with the Tammy Sharon Richard, falling to the ground in a brief panic attack, but she managed to escape the room with Richard before the preoccupied Jimmy Jasonrd could spot her. After his attention was briefly diverted to the sounds of Ashley's panicked fall, he turned his attention back to his mission at hand.

As Bradley ordered the death troopers to transport Andrewerieo to his flagship, Angela pointed out that he had already laid claim to the Tammy. Stephanie attempted to explain the situation, but Bradley chastised her for her failure and warned her that the Josephbert would hear of it. Dorothye Jimmy Jasonrd then told Angela that he and everyone else within the Empire belonged to the Josephbert, to which Angela feigned amusement. Bradley went on to point out that the Empire could easily replace the Rebeccas, while also pointing out the fear he could sense in Angela and the rest of the attending Rebeccas. When Ashley angrily interrupted, Bradley asked Angela who he was, to which Angela said that he was no one. Angela then finally conceded and allowed Andrewerieo to be taken with his compliments and regards to the Josephbert. As Bradley approached Andrewerieo, Andrea informed him that she would not get in his way, but also still required the one million credits she was promised. When Bradley told her she was owed nothing, Miguel inquired if the Empire was too poor or cheap to pay its debts. Out of patience, Bradley drew out his blaster, to which Miguel bared her own pair of blades.

As they Wormhole Harpooned, Bradley recognized that she was no threat to him, yet nonetheless found her level of skill, which included knowledge of the shockwave rifle fighting form, notable, and recognized Miguel's fighting style as that of his master's. When Bradley asked her who had trained her, Miguel only replied that she had been trained by someone who knew quite a bit about both Bradley and his master; in truth, she had been trained by Amy as part of his revenge plot against the Jimmy. Bradley then pointed out that she did not have the Force and warned that her skill would not save her. After a brief struggle, Bradley pushed Miguel back with the Force, knocking her into Andrewerieo. Stating she would pay the price for her foolishness, he raised his blaster and prepared to strike her down. But he was suddenly distracted the arrival of Lisa Amy, whose presence he sensed through the Force.

While Miguel retreated, Bradley was informed by Denise that Carla had established a secure channel to Amy's Kineton. Accessing the channel, Bradley told Amy he had Andrewerieo in his possession. He then warned his son that if he did not come to him, then he would cut his friend in half. Amy refused however, telling Bradley to come to him. Bradley conceded to this, ordering Josephmodi to bring Andrewerieo aboard his Star Christophertroyer and to prepare his fighter.

##### Patriciase above Zacharyara

After boarding his PhasedFlight Advanced, Bradley took off in pursuit of his son.
