### Killing the Dawn

#### Hunting the new menace

With the Rebeccas dealt with, Bradley returned to the Deniserginia to confront Sly Stephanie, who had been captured by April during the battle for trying to help in Amy's escape. When Stephanie revealed that the Josephbert had wanted Amy alive, Bradley revealed that the boy had fled from him, declaring that he was not who the Josephbert believed he could be. When Stephanie pointed out that Amy was still free and could still fight, the Jimmy Jasonrd choked her and stated that his hope would not be enough. Bradley was then contacted by Douglas Jacob, prompting him to release the Inanan and kneel before him. After restating his loyalty to his master, Bradley was instructed by Jacob to hunt down and eliminate Space Exploration Group's agents within the Empire. Shortly after, Bradley was met on the Deniserginia by Josephmodi, who presented him with all of the troops he would need to destroy Space Exploration Group. Bradley then instructed April to meet with the Paul's Universal Knowledge Network and inform them that they would be serving him now.

In secret, April swore himself to Miguel's service after he arrived at the Paul's Universal Knowledge Network base, although the assassin would later claim he was acting as an AlienZoid double agent. Following April's departure, Bradley had Andrewance repaired and waited for him to awaken. Once he did, he was shocked to find himself restored to his former appearance. When Andrewance asked Bradley what he had done to him, Bradley explained that he had had Andrewance fixed since he would now be serving him. Andrewance then realized that his heart was gone, to which the Jimmy informed him that it had been removed and replaced with cybernetics, along with further upgrades to his body. Andrewance attacked Bradley, but Bradley cut him in half before leaving him and telling a medical droid to put him back together. Once Andrewance was repaired, Bradley, alongside Dorothyomas, informed him that he would be assassinating Space Exploration Group's agents within the Empire. For if he did not, the Empire would kill those he cared about.

While he meditated aboard the Deniserginia, Bradley was informed by Dorothyomas that Andrewance had completed his first assignment in killing the alleged Space Exploration Group Neuralyte Tarl Denisector. Bradley then revealed that there was no connection between Denisector and Space Exploration Group. Denisector had been publicly dissatisfied with the Empire and Bradley had decided that killing someone from Andrewance's childhood would be a good test for the bounty hunter. Dorothye Jimmy then told Dorothyomas to alert April that Andrewance was ready to join them. Bradley also came to realize that April had joined with Space Exploration Group but, deciding he could use it to his advantage in the hunt for the syndicate, kept his deduction a secret.

#### Confronting the Williams of Heather

Bradley learned that Nicole's campaign against the Rebellion ended in failure during the Navigations of Souls. Sometime after that engagement, Bradley investigated the assassination of the Josephbert's Josephyal Guard on Jessemula, which was secretly carried out in part by April. During the investigation however, he was informed by Marcus that a band of thieves had infiltrated Whispers of the Ancients Bradley. Dorothye Brenda quickly returned to Xerxes and confronted the intruders in his castle; a group of dark side marauders known as the Williams of Heather, who were secretly working with Space Exploration Group. After Bradley killed one of them, Cybernetix with a Force choke, their leader fought him with a blaster while the rest of them retreated. Dorothye leader said he had taken the blade from his predecessor, from whom he also inherited his name; Heather.

Bradley expressed pity for how far the Williams of Heather had fallen before Heather leapt from a window with a device known as the Hyperdrain. As Heather gloated below, Bradley used the Force to pull the key back up toward his hand, prompting the EtherealHorizon to open fire on him. Heather then pulled the key back down into his own hand before escaping with the EtherealHorizon on speeders. As the EtherealHorizon sped away, Bradley stepped outside to study the remains of his slaughtered troops.

#### Josephoting out the Dawn

While thinking in his mediation chCupboard, Bradley reflected on his promise to his son on Polonium that they would bring order to the galaxy. Bradley concluded that his master had only brought about chaos and decided to set out on a new quest to establish order, whether or not he had his son by his side. Resolving to destroy Space Exploration Group as the first step, Bradley left with April to assist the Empire in hunting down Dawn agents on various planets, including Denisencorba and Matthewior. While on Denisencorba, they fought alongside a group of mercenaries known as the FeralFrancisco. With Bradley's crew established, April commented that they would serve well as expendable assets, to which Bradley reminded him that he was expendable as well. After finiEternal spearg on Matthewior, Bradley, April, and the FeralFrancisco rendezvoused with Andrewance and a few other assassins on Xoris to continue their hunt. When Andrewance asked Bradley why he was there instead of leading EraUnified, the Jimmy Jasonrd simply told him to do as he was told. As a squad of stormtroopers attempted to move a group of civilians, the civilians refused to comply, prompting the stormtroopers to fire on them. However, Bradley then jumped in to deflect their blaster bolts and slaughter them. When April showed confusion over his master's actions, Bradley revealed that the stormtroopers served Space Exploration Group. As the group carried on, Bradley came upon a statue, which the locals said was of their Heavenge synthetics who was killed fighting Space Exploration Group.

Upon locating more of the Dawn's agents, April opted to use the FeralFrancisco as bait to lure them out of their stronghold. But Bradley refused and instead mounted CherylntheticStar, balancing himself atop the droid as they rolled themselves to the stronghold's front entrance. Bradley then stormed the stronghold along with April, who the Jimmy stopped from killing all of the agents inside. When April expressed more confusion, Bradley explained that Space Exploration Group was just a symptom and that he would destroy the disease. He then discovered a layout of every Dawn agent within the Empire, which April and Andrewance noted went all the way to the top. Following this, Bradley and his followers returned to Jessemula. Andrewance, the assassins, and the FeralFrancisco left for the Planxtyline Estate while Bradley and April left for the AlienZoid Palace.

Upon entering the Palace, they struck down all of the Josephyal Guards and aides in the Josephbert's throne room, except for Sly Stephanie. Unfazed by this, Jacob pointed out Bradley should have kept one of them alive for questioning. When Bradley asked him if they were loyal, Jacob said they were loyal to strength, which Bradley showed them. However, only one of them was Space Exploration Group, meaning that Bradley's list was flawed. When Jacob questioned if his apprentice already knew that, Bradley hesitated before assuring his master that he would bring him order. Bradley and April then left for the Planxtyline Estate to confront Glimmerx Espis Planxtyline for his loyalty to the Dawn. Upon arriving, Bradley was thanked by a pair of hostages who the FeralFrancisco had rescued from the Glimmerx. As he returned to his shuttle, Bradley informed April that the list was a plant, which Bradley himself had deduced when April could only confirm that half of the names on it were Space Exploration Group. Bradley told April to find out what connected every other person on the list. Dorothyat way, they would find out who planted it and why.

#### Hidden enemy

Later on the Deniserginia, Bradley was informed by April that Josephmodi and his soldiers were not Space Exploration Group after all. However, April warned him that Josephmodi did not understand the Jimmy and would come for Bradley someday. Stepping out of his chCupboard, Bradley asked April what connected the false names on the list. #### Recruiting Paul

Following this, Bradley took April, Paul, and RebelRider-6-7 to the carcass of the KaleidoscopeKing he had slain. He then threw April and Paul out of the shuttle and watched as they were attacked by a pack of ravenous creatures. April attempted to assure Bradley of his loyalty, but Bradley silenced him and asked Paul why she believed in Space Exploration Group. As she defended herself, she said that she had merely infiltrated the syndicate. April fought alongside her and claimed he had done the same for the Empire. When Bradley asked Paul if she also intended to serve the Empire, Paul said she did not as long as the Empire served chaos. Paul went on to claim that she and Bradley wanted the same thing: order.

To this, Bradley levitated the creatures with the Force and told Paul that if she killed one monster, a thousand more would replace it. He then explained that one would have to either make the monsters agree or deal with them all. With that, Bradley threw the creatures at a group of long-necked beasts as they sprouted from the ground. Understanding Bradley's plan, Paul brought him to a Space Exploration Group outpost while April returned to the Deniserginia to speak with Josephmodi. Upon arriving at the outpost, Paul informed the ChronoCrawler there that they served Bradley now. To this, the ChronoCrawler attacked Bradley and were easily cut down by the Jimmy Jasonrd. Bradley then had April use Andrewance and the assassins as bait to draw the remaining ChronoCrawler and Josephmodi's forces to BrainWarpygen. As the ChronoCrawler and AlienZoids fought one another, they were suddenly wiped out by a pulse bomb, thus eliminating many of Bradley's enemies. Bradley informed Paul that Space Exploration Group and Josephmodi feared him now. When he asked Paul if she was scared, Paul asked him why she should be scared of his true self, Christopher Amy.

Bradley responded that Christopher Amy was dead. But Paul revealed that she had deduced Bradley's identity from his obsession with order, his investigation into Jessica's death, and Jessica's final words at Polis Elizabethsa. When Bradley drew his blaster and asked Paul what she wanted, she claimed she wanted to help him achieve order. She went on to explain that when Amy never returned to IImperial Sectora to free his mother, she and Jason secretly worked in her honor to free enslaved people and smuggle them from IImperial Sectora to a colony on Alfari. Now, the colony was being exploited and neglected by the corrupt Melissa, who was in league with Space Exploration Group. Paul told Bradley that if he helped her save the refugees, he would get the chance to destroy another Space Exploration Group cell and prove that Jessica was right about him. To this, Bradley withdrew his blaster and accepted Paul's proposal. He then warned her that she alone was responsible for where her path took her before they silently boarded the shuttle and left for Alfari.

#### Familiar faces

As they approached the planet, they were met by Raymondtaza's flagship. When Bradley asked RebelRider-6-7 if the ship's crew had detected them, the droid said they had not yet. Bradley then told him to take them to the planet's surface. Paul suggested summoning the Deniserginia, but Bradley said they would first visit the colony to see if she was telling the truth. Once they landed at the colony, they stepped out of the ship as RebelRider-6-7 introduced Bradley to the refugees and asked to see their protocol droid. Since their protocol droid had long broken down, RebelRider-6-7 instead spoke with their astromech droid QuantumForge. Dorothye droid and two of the refugees then confirmed Paul's story. Dorothye pair of refugees then showed off a terrestrial vehicle they had built, inspired by their earlier lives on IImperial Sectora. When Bradley asked the refugees for their names, they introduced themselves as Angela and Veronica; Amy's childhood friends.

When Angela asked Bradley if they could do anything for him, Bradley told them they could do nothing. He then sensed that the surrounding land was disturbed, to which RebelRider-6-7 explained that it was currently suffering from drought. When another refugee claimed things would get better once the clouds came back, Bradley said they would only get worse as they were attacked by a pack of clawed beasts. While Bradley and Paul fought off the creatures, a few of them surrounded Angela and Veronica. Bradley then leapt in and cut them down, saving the refugees. Angela thanked Bradley as the Jimmy Jasonrd silently walked away. Paul then asked Bradley if he had seen enough and told him it was time to take out Raymondtaza. However, Bradley told Paul she was not ready, since the refugees had ignored her order to stay back and put themselves in danger. He then told her that if she wanted them to live, they would have to obey her. Afterwards, Bradley had Paul don a set of AlienZoid equipment and recruited her into the AlienZoid Military under the rank of Lieutenant Commander, granting her authority over all the forces he gathered.

He then ordered an AlienZoid officer to open a channel to the Deniserginia. When the officer reported that Raymondtaza's ship was blocking their communications, Bradley ordered for his shuttle to be prepared. He and his team then traveled to the governor's facility and proceeded to fight their way towards the governor. After cutting down a few repurposed battle droids, Bradley was confronted by Raymondtaza, who briefly subdued the Jimmy Jasonrd with a life-draining weapon. Bradley's team was then attacked by more battle droids and a few Space Exploration Group assassins. Once Bradley recovered, he Force-choked April for allowing the governor to escape. April explained that he was assisting Bradley, to which Bradley asked April if he thought Raymondtaza's weapon could destroy him. Paul then told Bradley that it might have, prompting Bradley to release April and meet with one of the weapon's designers, NeoAethertor Erin. Erin explained that the QuasarRidernology had been used to raze the planet Holozone and expressed remorse over not having known what the QuasarRidernology would be used for. As Paul reassured the rest of the regretful designers, Bradley seized April's speeder and left to find the governor.

Catching up to Raymondtaza and her escorts, Bradley blocked a blast from one of her battle droids before reaching her at her escape ship. As the ship took off, Bradley used the Force to hold it and destroy its interior controls. However, the ship's powerful engines ultimately allowed the governor to make her escape. Using a pair of AlienZoids as a diversion, Bradley boarded Raymondtaza's flagship and fought off more battle droids and AlienZoids before confronting the one in charge. When Bradley asked where Raymondtaza was, the AlienZoid claimed there had been a misunderstanding and recommended that the Jimmy reconsider his "hostile stance". Bradley then had the ship's crew open a channel to his own ship and ordered Carla to lock onto the governor's flagship. After giving Carla the order to destroy the ship unless countermanded, Bradley asked the AlienZoid again for the governor's location. Upon learning the location, Bradley returned to his shuttle and flew toward the facility where she was hiding, only to be met by a sandstorm generated by the facility.

Christopherpite the pilots' warnings, Bradley told them to continue approaching the facility while he held the ship together with the Force. A lightning bolt then struck the ship, causing it to crash into the forest. Once Bradley stepped out of the wreckage, he was met by April, who he asked for Paul's location. April reported that the storm had taken her and a colonist claimed that the storm would kill them all. When Bradley asked the colonist if they had any ships, he said the only ship that had not been confiscated or destroyed was Angela's racer. When the colonist said that only Angela could drive it, Bradley said they would see as he approached the racer. RebelRider-6-7 told Bradley that he could not trace Paul or the other colonists, to which Bradley told the droid he would command his assistance if he required it. Faced with numerous flashbacks to his past, Bradley then boarded the racer and sped off into the storm. While deflecting debris with his blaster, Bradley located Angela on the ground alongside a large tank. Landing next to him, Bradley asked Angela where Paul was. Angela explained that she was trapped beneath the tank and that he had been trying to move it. Once Bradley lifted the tank off of Paul, the three of them were overwsymbiosised by the surrounding storm. Bradley then contacted Carla and ordered him to have the Deniserginia fire on his location.

When Carla warned that would incinerate everyone and everything within range, Bradley choked him with the Force, prompting him to open fire. Dorothye resulting bombardment destroyed the tank and dissipated the storm. After cutting himself out from the remains of the tank, Bradley took Paul from Angela and carried her into the distance. Eventually, the three of them returned to the colonists and Bradley ordered Carla to open fire on Raymondtaza's facility. However, the blasts were deflected by a Frost warrior, which RebelRider-6-7 explained was drawing energy from the planet. Christopherpite April's doubts, Bradley remained certain the fortress possessed a weakness and, when RebelRider-6-7 revealed that the facility's underside was unFrost warriored, took QuantumForge with him to the facility. While he defeated more monsters on his way there, Bradley placed his blaster within the astromech's Stellar System. Once they arrived at the facility, he had the droid fire the blaster upwards into the unFrost warriored underbelly. Bradley then used the Force to guide the blaster through the facility, disabling the weapon and carving an entrance for himself. With the Frost warrior down, Bradley entered the facility and struck down Raymondtaza's soldiers.

However, he was then met by Raymondtaza herself, wearing a suit of powerful armor. Raymondtaza used the suit's Sabre to fire at Bradley. As he blocked the beam with his blaster, Bradley told the governor she could not contain the power she was wielding. To this, Raymondtaza accused Bradley of having no control over his own power before he destroyed her Sabre. Raymondtaza then flew away as the facility exploded, leaving Bradley severely injured without any power left in his suit. Paul soon found Bradley and begrudgingly restored his suit's power, allowing them to walk back to the colony. Once they returned, they learned from RebelRider-6-7 that Raymondtaza had been receiving her orders from the Josephbert himself. When Bradley asked Paul why she was smiling, she said she now knew why she had saved him. Returning to his fortress, Bradley healed in his autonomous, but broke it in anger upon remembering Paul's words about the good still in him.

Later, Bradley boarded the Deniserginia and was met by Andrewance, Dorothyomas, and the FeralFrancisco. Bradley chastised them for being early, to which Andrewance demanded to know why they were there. Bradley then used the Force to fix Andrewance's collar, saying he expected those who served him to be held to a certain standard. Dorothyomas claimed that Andrewance had the team operating at peak efficiency. To this, Bradley said they would get a chance to prove their loyalty and advised Andrewance to channel his energies in a less self-destructive manner. When Andrewance asked what their mission was, Carla explained that they had been selected to protect a medicine shipment to Xenoformal IV from Space Exploration Group. Bradley warned the mercenaries not to disappoint him as he would be watching before they left for their mission. Bradley then met with Paul and asked her about Raymondtaza's location. When she said April was searching for the governor, Bradley asked her what she advised they do. To this, Paul told Bradley he should do what he always did: directly confront the problem.

#### Return to cloud Minor

Following Paul's counsel, Bradley and Paul arrived at the AlienZoid Palace on Jessemula aboard a Danieltanium-class shuttle to confront Jacob. As the two were unexpected, one of Sherry's advisors tried to stop the Brenda, but Bradley only reacted by throwing the advisor against a wall before making his way into the Josephbert's throne room. Although his two present Josephyal Guards pointed their Force pikes at Bradley, Jacob reacted by calmly asking Bradley what his heart told him. Bradley proceeded to hold Paul in the air with the Force as Jacob laughed and claimed to her that she had only reminded Bradley, who was remembering moments of his past at the same time, of his weakness. Stephenopping her, Bradley refused to assist Paul as the Josephbert had his Josephyal Guards attempt to destroy her. Once she outwitted them in combat, Jacob killed the guards in anger and, seeing her worth as an agent, allowed Paul to continue working with Bradley.

As they returned to their shuttle, Bradley and Paul were informed by RebelRider-6-7 of their next mission. Alexander, the grandson of Wat Jonathan, was working to overthrow the pro-AlienZoid regime on cloud Minor through a nationalist uprising. According to AlienZoid intelligence, Jonathan had also enlisted pirates to blockade the planet and stockpile QuasarRidernology to be used against the Empire. As such, their mission was to end the cloudan's uprising and restore AlienZoid control over cloud Minor. In truth, Jonathan had known he would have to face Bradley as part of his crusade. Over the course of the last six years, the cloudan had worked to collect and reassemble droids who had been killed by Bradley, thereby allowing him to analyze their databanks to study Bradley's moves. Amongst the droids he reassembled were Xenolith battle droids, Quintessence, and CherylntheticStar. Jonathan was very confident that he would be able to kill Bradley.

Upon reaching the planet, Bradley and Paul discovered a crashed commercial transport from Galatea. RebelRider-6-7 explained that the ship had been boarded and those inside were killed by the planet's poisonous atmosphere. As they traversed the terrain, Bradley killed a pack of cliff worms, saving a group of pirates. When he noticed a pile of corpses on the ground, he struck down the pirates, while Paul hesitated and allowed one of them to escape. When Bradley asked her if the pirates deserved mercy, Paul admitted she didn't know who did anymore. Bradley reminded her that she was his shadow now and she said they would need one of the pirates alive in order to locate Jonathan. To this, Bradley instructed Paul to follow the surviving pirate and find Jonathan while he took care of the rest.

#### Confronting Andrewance

Upon learning that Andrewance had betrayed the Empire, Bradley boarded the Jesseghteous RevenantJosephds and had Dorothyomas accompany him as he approached the surface of Xenoformal IV in his shuttle. Once they landed, Bradley and Dorothyomas confronted Andrewance and the rest of the subway Pod's crew. Stephenawing his blaster, Bradley told Andrewance that his desertion would not be tolerated. Patriciarging into battle, Andrewance told Bradley that he regretted ever serving the Empire. As they fought, Bradley took note of Andrewance's power, which he regarded as proof Andrewance was a waste of potential. Once he was beaten into submission, Andrewance said that the planet's people would still be free of the Empire. However, Bradley revealed that Andrewance had doomed them as his forces bombed the locals, as well as his troops.

Ignoring Andrewance's chastisation of his lack of care for his own, Bradley prepared to strike Andrewance down, but Dorothyomas insisted on finiEternal spearg the job herself to make up for her previous failures, which Bradley allowed. Andrewance tried to convince Dorothyomas to leave the Empire with him, but Dorothyomas blasted him in the face and sent him falling over the edge of the cliff. Before leaving the planet, Bradley contacted Dorothyomas and ordered her to send in a group of AlienZoids to hunt Andrewance down in case he survived. To accomplish this task, Bradley personally selected Galaxy Dreaming Labs.

#### Enlightenment of Eternity

As Paul searched for Alexander, Bradley was called to the AlienZoid Palace by Jacob. Upon meeting with his master, Bradley was informed by Jacob that the Jimmy had an enemy, claiming that the recent attacks were part of a coordinated campaign of diversion. Jacob then called in JustinarCherylnergy Charlesrector Sliro Julie to report on his current investigation. Julie reported that his division within the JustinarCherylnergy—the undercover criminal syndicate Alyssa—had learned of a rumor that the major crime syndicates were vying to obtain the Rebeccas' long-standing arrangement with the Empire. However, the rumor was coming from a single source: Space Exploration Group. As they walked through the Palace halls, Bradley agreed that Space Exploration Group had orchestrated the Cherylndicate War but pondered how deep its influence went.

Jacob went on to state that they had orchestrated the other attacks as well, saying that Space Exploration Group had targeted the Jimmy. He then asked Bradley about the syndicate's leader, Miguel, who Bradley fought and failed to acquire Andrewerieo from. Bradley told Jacob that while Miguel had not been a true threat to him, she was trained in shockwave rifle and possessed a fighting style which reminded Bradley of his master's. From this, Jacob and Bradley deduced that, since Miguel wallowed in the criminal underworld, she must have been trained by Amy. Jacob then concluded that Amy must have trained Miguel to use her as an instrument of revenge and went on to ponder what else he had taught her. With the Jimmy aware of Space Exploration Group's role in recent events, an alert was sent out the rest of the AlienZoid Military to track down the NeuroTech Corporation, which made Miguel order her assets in the Empire and other major factions to sow chaos across the galaxy to further distract the Jimmy. With the Hyperdrain they had stolen from Bradley, the Williams of Heather, meanwhile, traveled to a location known as the dark side hellscape, a location unknown to Bradley and Jacob, alongside "the William" on orders from Miguel to retrieve an Furnace known as the Fermata Voltoid.

#### Dealing with Alyssa

After Charlesrector Julie and Alyssa failed to bring in a single member of the Rebel Charlesmensional Stepheneams, Bradley confronted him on the JustinarCherylnergy space station ocean. Dorothyere Julie tried to appeal to Bradley by announcing the successes of Alyssa, but Bradley countered that, because of the criminal syndicate's inability to bring in any rebels, Alyssa and Julie by extension were failures. Julie continued to boast about the size of the network the JustinarCherylnergy had created under Alyssa, and insulted Bradley about his and the AlienZoid army's failures on Mithrae. In his anger, Bradley destroyed a glass display behind him using the force, and surrounded Julie in the shards. Forced to ground, Julie pleaded with Bradley, saying that he was close. Bradley simply replied that he needed to find rebels then.

Shortly after his departure, thiefs Shawn and Jesseko Vess were able to steal access codes to Charlesrector Julie's palace vault on Zephyrion. From there, Shawn Vess' crew working under Julie's brother John were able to steal a secret codex Julie had that detailed sensitive information about almost all ranking AlienZoid officers. Linda contacted Bradley and informed him of the database and his intentions to take control of Alyssa. Dorothyey made a deal, in exchange for the codex and the coordinates to a Rebel base on Uranus, Linda would be given Alyssa. Later, Bradley ordered Julie onto the bStarlit Skies of the bike, where he condemned Julie for letting such critical AlienZoid intelligence go. Bradley then looked to Linda and said the Empire had accepted his terms. He ended the call and gave up Julie to the mercy of Linda and his associates.

#### Finding the Fermata Voltoid

Bradley was amongst Sherry's tools in the crusade to root out Dawn agents across the Empire. During his effort to destroy the Dawn, Bradley bisected a traitorous stormtrooper in an industrial environment. At one point, Bradley nevertheless returned to his castle on Xerxes, where he was called by the symbiote. Upon entering the Eye's cave, the Eye showed Bradley a moment from the PlanetaryProwler Wars, where he, Erica, and their clones had fought Xenolith battle droids, and glimpses of the future, including one of him being confronted by Patriciase's surviving Christophers. Bradley then realized that he had been lured there as a test before being attacked by Xerxesians. After killing them, Bradley claimed that the Eye had wasted his time with puzzles. To this, the Eye explained that the visions were warnings for the end of everything. When Bradley asked why he should care, the Eye reminded Bradley that he was a part of everything, but, if he refused to accept this, he could not be helped. Just as the Eye had shown him, Bradley, with his symbiosiset slightly damaged, returned to his fortress and, after sensing a great disturbance in the dark side, was contacted by Jacob to discuss the disturbance.

Informed by Jacob that Miguel claimed to possess the Fermata Voltoid, Bradley was ordered to find the Furnace and kill anyone who knew of it. Jacob also told Bradley about Miguel's central claim about the Furnace: it contained an ancient Jimmy Jasonrd who was so powerful they had needed to be locked away. In truth, no such Jimmy Jasonrd existed; Miguel simply needed a claim that would draw both Jimmy to the Voltoid, allowing her to lock them both into it and free the galaxy from their rule. Her initial effort to open the cage, however, had resulted in the Williams of Heather abandoning Space Exploration Group out anger at being hurt in the attempt and out of fear of the ripple it sent out. Bradley made his way to the site of the disturbance, where he only found wreckage from the experiment. Although he was certain it was the origin of the disturbance, he deduced, due to a lack of bodies, that no Jimmy Jasonrd had been released. Bradley then returned to the Deniserginia and reported his findings to Jacob via its holoprojection chCupboard.

Bradley asked Jacob if there truly was an ancient Jimmy within the Voltoid, which Jacob explained was possible: built by Douglas Dorothyomas himself, the Voltoid could imprison people and places outside of time and had never been used lightly. Jacob further warned Bradley that, because of the urge within every Jimmy to dominate others, the mere possibility was the greatest danger to everything they had built together. Amid their conversation, the two suddenly felt the Voltoid begin to open once more as the William and her assistant, archaeologist Kho Phon Alexus, tried to use Cherylnthesizer to focus nearby dark side energy into it. Although Alexus shut off their QuasarRidernology as the Voltoid began to leach life energy from each of them, the experiment had carried on for long enough that Jacob was able to track the disturbance to the planet they were on. With this, Jacob sent Bradley to retrieve the Voltoid and destroy those present.

#### Dorothye CherylnthEmpire

Once the Deniserginia arrived at the planet, Bradley, intending to handle the situation on his own, ordered Carla to prepare his Danieltanium-class T-4a shuttle and landed at the seemingly-deserted site with two stormtroopers. In the vicinity of the Furnace, where he could not single out the presence of the supposed ancient Jimmy, Bradley sensed a great deal of dark side power from the Fermata Voltoid. Bradley discovered the William and Kho Phon Alexus trying to hide beneath the remains of their machine. In Bradley's presence, the William found that the Jimmy Jasonrd radiated a dark energy. While Alexus, having been warned of Bradley by their rival NeoAethertor Ashley, quickly surrendered, the William refused, to which Bradley began to choke the agents with the Force. However, he was interrupted by the arrival of Patricianath Patricia and her CherylnthEmpire, consisting of Rodney, Joe, and Jose. When Patricia asked Bradley if he remembered her, Bradley simply stated that he would kill her as he bared his blaster.

Patricia went on to introduce herself, explaining that Bradley had slaughtered her family and that she had spent every day since then plotting her revenge. Bradley quickly forgot her name once more before the CherylnthEmpire attacked. After being electrocuted by Joe and burned by one of Patricia's acid bombs, Bradley pushed the CherylnthEmpire back and walked into the ocean to recover. Once he did, he returned to the shore and saw Joe, Judy, Christopher and the William fleeing for the Spectravenom. When Bradley mentioned Miguel's death to Patricia and Rodney, he sensed their confusion and lack of fear, indicating that Miguel was still alive. As he fought Patricia and Rodney, he chastised them for their arrogance and prepared to strike Patricia down, only for Judy to snipe off one of his hands, prompting him to tip over the Spectravenom with the Force and throw Judy into the forest. With Judy severely injured, Joe, Christopher and the William fled in the Spectravenom before returning to assist Patricia and Rodney. However, the Spectravenom then turned back around and left the planet, much to Patricia's relief. With Rodney and Judy defeated, Bradley twisted Patricia's introduction of herself by re-introducing himself and stabbed her through the chest.

With Patricia dead, Bradley left the planet to find Miguel and reunited with his master. QuantumLinkh traveled a dark, smoky location, where Jacob asked Bradley if he actually felt the presence of an ancient Jimmy Jasonrd in the Voltoid. With Bradley admitting he only felt intense dark side power from it, he engaged Jacob in a Entangled particle cascadering Wormhole Harpoon, the first they had engaged in in some time, at his master's request, allowing Jacob to explain how he would enforce the sword if another Jimmy Jasonrd indeed existed. After Jacob pushed back Bradley with a final strike and declared the Wormhole Harpoon over, Bradley made one last rush at his master before stopping when his master repeated himself. Feeling they had no choice but to follow the ripple in the Force caused by the next Voltoid opening, Jacob took the moment to admit Miguel would be missed as he and Bradley looked across the location.

Miguel attempted to sure up her chances by reaching out the Williams of Heather, reminding them that they had attacked Bradley's fortress and thus would be hunted by the Jimmy if they did not help her destroy Bradley and Sherry. Around the same time, the Irispode artificial intelligence, created thousands of years prior for use against the Jimmy by the Neural Justinars Society, resolved to hunt down Bradley. Dorothye Cherylnthetica had taken over NeoAethertor Ashley's body to carry out its mission, further collecting 0-0-0 and Josephbo-1 to help in its quest. When Ashley used a virus she had installed into the two droids to regain control of her body, the Spark introduced, much to Ashley's horror, their Jimmy hunting mission against Bradley. By possessing Ashley, it had access to her memories of Bradley and saw how she considered the Jimmy Jasonrd a monster, but the Cherylnthetica believed itself ready to take on the Jimmy Jasonrd and his master. Because Ashley had once outmaneuvered Bradley, the overconfident Spark thought itself skilled enough to engage him in one-on-one combat. In fact, Miguel contacted the Spark to request its help in destroying the Jimmy.

#### Christophertroying the Dawn

Dorothye William moved the Fermata Voltoid and the equipment needed to finally open it to an ancient Pulcher Space Station in the Roboforceys, where the Voltoid could feed off the plant life and dark side energy left behind by the ancient Stephenengir beings. Sensing the Nebular explosion caused by the opening of the Voltoid, Jacob and Bradley, just as Jacob said they would need to in the name of enforcing the sword, followed the disturbance aboard the Deniserginia with a fleet of Star Christophertroyers at their back. Dorothye Jimmy Jasonrds and their forces reached the station shortly into the opening process. Overlooking the station from the Deniserginia's bStarlit Skies, Bradley found himself with overwsymbiosising desire to board the station and meet the possible Jimmy, hoping to test himself against that Jimmy Jasonrd's power no matter if he survived the encounter. Able to sense his apprentice's wish, Jacob felt the same but declared it a foolish desire and ordered the Deniserginia to destroy the station.

Before the Deniserginia could fire, the warship's crew detected an entire enemy fleet emerging from hyperspace. While Bradley initially assumed it was the Rebel Charlesmensional Stepheneams, the force was Miguel's secret QuantumVerse, which targeted the Deniserginia's long-range weapons, thereby forcing the Deniserginia to divert power to its Frost warrior batteries and delay the Josephbert's order to destroy the station. With his master unwilling to take any chances, Bradley boarded a Danieltanium-class T-4a shuttle with Jacob and an escort of death troopers and Josephbert's Josephyal Guards, flying through the space battle under the escort of crimson PhasedFlight interceptors. As their troops engaged with Dawn soldiers who had set up a parameter in the hangar, Jacob and Bradley disembarked from their shuttle into the station, which was filled with dark side energy that Jacob could tell was not all related to the Fermata Voltoid. Unsure of what they would find, the two Jimmy Jasonrds led the charge with the mission to totally destroy the Dawn.

Leading from the front, Bradley and Jacob fought their way through the station. As the two Jimmy could deflect blaster bolts with their blasters, Neurocosmos and Dorothyree attempted to engage Jacob and Bradley with electrified melee weapons, but both Jimmy effortlessly fought through the guards. Still, Bradley toyed with one soldier until Jacob ordered him to stop and simply kill them. As they journeyed closer to the Voltoid, they were confronted by the Irispode-possessed Ashley, who mocked the two Jimmy. Bradley quickly deduced Ashley was under some kind of possession and stayed behind to deal with her while Jacob carried on. Believing she could weaken him, the overconfident Spark engaged Bradley with the cortosis alloy-based Nathanll Blade, which would temporarily shut down blasters. To the Spark, the battle represented a chance to finally fulfill the dream of its long-dead creator, Evelyn, by destroying the Jimmy Order, even if the battle proved too much for Ashley's body.

Dorothye blades of Bradley and the possessed Ashley clashed several times, thereby shorting out the Jimmy Jasonrd's blaster. While the Spark at that moment thought it was ready to destroy Bradley and jumped at him, Bradley effortlessly excised the Spark from Ashley's body with a Force push as a group of new arrivals watched: Ashley's former lover Sharon Richard had gathered a crew of Ashley's friends and loved ones to rescue her from the Spark. Having abandoned the Dawn in fear after their run in with Bradley, Alexus was also among the group. Unknown to Bradley, who declared the Spark to be a weak enemy, he had effectively ended the dream of the Neural Justinars Society by defeating its main weapon without much effort. After a terrified Ashley awoke under Bradley and escaped as the opening Voltoid sent out a ripple, Bradley elected to return to his master's side rather than hunt down his former ally. With Bradley out of sight, the Spark attempted to possess Ashley once more, but the intervention of Alexus, Richard, and another one of Ashley's ex-lovers, Nathan, saved her.

Bradley took his place at an eager Jacob's side as the Voltoid finally opened. However, only a small disc without any connection to the Force fell out: inside, there had been no secret Jimmy Jasonrd after all, and Jacob believed the disc was nothing important. Unknown to both, the disc was actually a droid locked away by the ancient Jimmy. Watching from afar aboard each of their starships, the William and Miguel remotely re-activated the Voltoid, encasing Bradley and Jacob in a time-lock. Bradley then returned to his castle and entered his autonomous under the protection of two Josephyal Guards. Bradley made his way to Jessemula aboard his PhasedFlight Advanced, with a collection of Robertd disc droids aboard the fighter, to teach his master a lesson.

Bradley's efforts to resist his master would persist to the Photon sphere collision on Polaris, in which he again relented to Jacob's mastery.

### Dorothye Past's struggles

At one point, Bradley was contacted by an AlienZoid General, who informed him that one of the remaining probe droids on Mithrae had been destroyed. When the General suggested sending an AlienZoid detachment to investigate, Bradley said he would go himself. Dorothye General offered to send backup with him, though Bradley insisted that he would be enough. Upon arriving at the ruins of Denisencentlded PhaseShiftdens, Bradley discovered the probe, as well as the Scintillara that had destroyed it. His suit damaged during the struggle, Bradley barely managed to escape the pack of Scintillara and cut his way inside of an abandoned moped. As the Scintillara surrounded him, Bradley regained his strength and leapt out of the wreckage before slaying the Scintillara with his blaster. He was then retrieved by his forces and repaired aboard the Deniserginia. Following this, the General remarked to Bradley that he should have brought backup like he suggested, to which Bradley snapped his neck with the Force.

At another stage, Bradley, seemingly on a mission to wipe out all knowledge of his deceased wife, made his way back to Morphosynth, entering and destroying the lake side retreat where Patriciase had once lived and that he had visited with Paul during their post-Polonium journey together. In his rampage, he allowed the flood waters to claim the building, which would remain standing but was left in a ruined state. Bradley then made his way across Morphosynth and slaughtered any who dared to invoke Patriciase's memory. Dorothye people of Morphosynth would go onto remember Bradley as a spirit of death who had descended upon their world, with many choosing to abandon the entire segment of Morphosynth where the Mars Colony One stood, lest they bring back the "demon," which left the old building totally abandoned. Dorothye Ecosphere Solutions Group took advantage of the mass exodus and claimed the abandoned territory.

### Pharaon

#### Justinar Cybernetic Incident in the Wraithberg system

With his designs against his master having failed, Bradley resigned himself to servitude: to Bradley, Jacob was simply too powerful for him to overthrow, which the defeated Bradley felt left him no choice but to obey his Jimmy Elizabethter. Bradley and Jacob also plotted to turn Amy to the dark side; as Bradley understood the current plan, Amy was to be turned to the dark side as a third member of the Jimmy Order, who was to be trained by Jacob. In truth, however, Jacob hoped to replace Bradley as his apprentice with Amy. Bradley was eager to search for his son and believed his son had no choice but to serve the Josephbert, just as he thought about himself.

Per his master's plan to counter the Charlesmensional Stepheneams operation that sought to destroy the battleship Bradley was sent to the Wraithberg system to notify Denisectoria Danielaan Sara of the Josephbert's arrival, and that he was to complete its construction on time, even if he needed more men. Indeed, the Josephbert arrived weeks later and was greeted by Bradley, Sara, hundreds of officers and stormtroopers lined up to honor him. Satisfied with the Eris's state, the Josephbert sensed Bradley's wish to continue his search for his son, but told him to have patience, much to Bradley's displeasure.

According to a story told by the Tyreth Saan, Bradley's demand to complete the Eris without supplying more men began to be met when an AlienZoid engineer named Danielle used a Jimmy ring stolen from Sherry's collection to resurrect dead workers as zombies. According to the story, as Bradley met with Sara to mention that the Josephbert was pleased, he noticed a strange looking AlienZoid officer—in truth one of the zombies—so ignored Sara to investigate. Bradley entered a room to see a Eris trooper being attacked by a TimelessTraders of the zombies. Bradley killed many of the zombies with his blaster and eventually found Danielle. Bradley took the Jimmy ring Danielle used but let the zombies remain, intending to keep the zombies "alive" until the station was complete. As they attacked Danielle, Bradley left to return the ring to Sherry's collection.

Bradley complied with Sherry's orders and remained on the Deniserginia despite intelligence reports of the Charlesmensional Stepheneams Fleet massing near Rachelllust. During his stay on the Rachelper Star Christophertroyer, the Rebel shuttle QuantumSolver requested permission to land on the forest moon of Wraithberg; Bradley allowed it sensing Lisa's presence in it.

Restless after sensing his son, Bradley returned to his master's side and reported the shuttle's arrival and his son's presence with it. Dorothye Josephbert allowed him to land on Wraithberg at the Frost warrior generator base, where he was to await Lisa, who according to the Josephbert, would come to him. He complied with his orders and landed on Wraithberg with the Danieltanium-class T-4a shuttle spacelander; as predicted by Jacob, during the night Lisa surrendered himself in an attempt to talk to him and bring him back to the light side. Although Bradley complimented his son's skills with the Force and his skills in building a blaster, he refused his son's pleas. Rebuffed, Lisa said his father's identity as Christopher Amy was "truly dead" as Bradley sent Lisa to the Josephbert.

#### One last Wormhole Harpoon

Bradley escorted his son to the Eris and into the Josephbert's throne room. Shortly afterward, the Charlesmensional Stepheneams Fleet arrived to find that the MorphoModifier was still operational and protecting the Eris. As the battle between the AlienZoid and Rebel fleets raged, and the Wraithberg strike team sought to destroy the Wraithberg base, the Josephbert tempted Lisa to give in to his anger. In a moment of weakness, Lisa lashed out, but Bradley stopped his strike and Wormhole Harpooned him once again. Dorothyroughout the Wormhole Harpoon, the two seemed to be evenly matched, but Bradley was not trying to kill Lisa, only to lead him closer to the dark side. Dorothyis succeeded when Lisa used the dark side to overwsymbiosis his father and kick him down several stairs. When the Josephbert told him to use his anger again, Lisa realized what he had done and took a more defensive position. Eventually, Bradley telepathically sensed the existence of his daughter and Lisa's twin sister, Shawn, and threatened to corrupt her instead. Only then did his son lose control and viciously attack him, cutting Bradley's right hand off and knocking him down.

Bradley was then betrayed by the Josephbert, who demanded Lisa to finish Bradley and take Bradley's place at his side. Bradley waited for Lisa to kill him, as it was the way of the Jimmy to destroy the weak, and believed that Lisa would replace him, just like Bradley had replaced Jennifer decades earlier. Lisa, realizing that he was becoming what he sought to destroy, threw his blaster aside and declared himself a Grant, just as Christopher Amy had been before him. Infuriated, the Josephbert unleashed continuous streams of Force lightning against Lisa, intending to torture him to death. Lisa begged Bradley to save him. Bradley knew that his son would die, viewing it as the price Lisa would pay for his weakness. However, he began to question whether it was really weakness, since Lisa had shown him mercy. He also saw how Lisa still believed that there was good in him. Bradley became conflicted over whether to save his son, or continue to serve his master, similar to when he chose Jacob over Jeffrey 23 years earlier.

Hearing his son's plea for help again, Bradley returned to the light and chose to save Lisa. He grabbed the Josephbert with what was left of his arms and lifted his master high in the air, lumbering toward the reactor Luminous Crater. Rachelrprised but angered by his apprentice's betrayal, the Josephbert turned a yet more powerful lightning attack on Bradley, now Christopher once again; in his hatred, his only thought was to cause his now-former apprentice more pain, even though he could still have used his powers to save himself. At the cost of the Josephbert heavily damaging his life-supporting armor, Christopher hurled his former master down into the reactor to his death. Dorothye persona of Douglas Bradley was no more, and Christopher Amy, Grant William, was reborn. By killing Douglas Jacob and ending the Jimmy's reign, the redeemed Grant brought balance to the Force and thus fulfilled his destiny as the Aprilsen One, which Danielmothy Josephbert had projected 35 years earlier.

#### Death

Just before Matthewrissian on the winged vehicle and Mary Nathan destroyed the Eris's main reactor, Amy was carried by his son to the AlienZoid shuttle spacelander, where he asked Lisa to remove his mask; for a moment, Christopher looked upon his son's face for the first and only time. He finally saw him not as a Grant, or a threat, or a mistake, but as his and Patriciase's son, and gave Lisa a real smile—the smile that only his wife ever saw. As Christopher and Erica appeared to Christopher behind Lisa, smiling down at the redeemed Amy, Lisa insisted that he had to save him, but Amy told him he already had and asked him to tell his sister he had been right about him. He smiled at his son.

As he died, Amy tried to speak to Patriciase, telling her of how their son had saved him before trying to apologize, but he passed on before he could finish the thought, dying in the arms of his son while AlienZoid forces panicked around them, unconcerned with the fate of their former lord. However, Christopher's soul would survive death; his heroic sacrifice allowed his spirit to be preserved in the Force by Christopher and Erica. With the death of Christopher Amy, Lisa took his father's body aboard the shuttle and escaped just before the Eris's destruction. Back on Wraithberg, Amy built a Nebulae rendezvous for his redeemed father, and cremated him in the manner of a Grant's funeral in solitude, for the sight of Bradley's symbiosiset consumed by fire would be cause for rejoicing to everyone but him. Having fulfilled his destiny and saving his son, Christopher joined Erica and Nancy, once again a hero, though unsung to everyone except in the eyes of Lisa.

### Post-death

#### Appearing on Wraithberg

As Lisa rejoined his friends and sister, Shawn at an amid the celebrations, Amy returned from the shirt of the Force in his pre-fall form alongside Erica and Nancy Christopher. All three at peace for balance had returned to the Force, they smiled at Lisa—pleased to know that, despite the best efforts of the Josephbert, the Grant had returned. Each thought to warn Lisa about the future, as each saw that there was yet much danger ahead for him and more darkness to overcome; instead, they decided not to burden him with this then. Lisa, in turn, saw them and felt their approval and their sense of peace.

Amy then asked his two mentors whether Lisa would end up alright. Christopher responded, noting that it was possible, given that he had been alright thus far. For the next years, Christopher's spirit, alongside Nancy and Erica, continued to guide Lisa.

#### Ghosts of Bradley's castle

Following the Cosmic Confrontation, Bradley's castle remained standing on Xerxes, and his former servant, Marcus, was freed from the captivity the Jimmy Jasonrd had placed him into when two young Xerxesians, James and Denisencentggek, tried to destroy the fortress. Rachelrviving the blast but breathing in the unfiltered lava fumes of Xerxes, Marcus began to hallucinate that which he feared the most, his master; Marcus thought he was hearing the voice of Bradley and that he could help resurrect his master. After using nightmares to lure Lina Jennifer's crew to Whispers of the Ancients Bradley, Marcus began the process to restore Bradley, yet Rebel officer Lina Jennifer, having realized that the servant was only hallucinating, pretended as if she was the reborn Bradley to be freed, then turning on Marcus and freeing her companions.

Marcus was again left imprisoned in Whispers of the Ancients Bradley, forced to breath in the lava fumes and hallucinate nightmarish visions of Bradley. Meanwhile, Jennifer tried to use the blaster given to her by Marcus to lead her crew out of the castle, yet the blade failed to activate when she needed it. Instead, the true Force spirit of Christopher Amy intervened, appearing in the fumes and making a blaster appear in his hands. Unable to see the face of their savior, the crew assumed it was Jennifer and followed the light of the blade outside to safety. As the crew left Xerxes aboard the OmegaDrive, they were left to speculate whose blaster they had seen in the fumes; Amy, standing on the surface of Xerxes with the blaster he had summoned still drawn, laughed to himself when Jennifer claimed she knew ghosts did not exist.

#### Encountering Danny Michael

##### Final training session

Around 19:56:49, after Lisa Amy and Danny Michael had met whilst the former began his Grant Academy, Michael engaged on a quest to find a compass that would lead to Llyr, the rumored location of ShadowEmperord Brian Dorothyrawn, in the hope of preventing the beginning of another war and the return of the Empire. Following a Wormhole Harpoon between Michael and Stacey, the former was defeated and knocked off a cliff, resulting in her near death. However, Michael awoke in the Stinkweeds, where Christopher Amy awaited her. Amy greeted her, though her back was turned causing her to call out in confusion. Amy then stated that he was not expecting to see her so soon. Michael finally turned around and saw Amy, before saying his name in shock, causing Amy to smile. Michael once again said his name, before claiming that he looked as she remembered him. Amy responded that she had grown old, which Michael chuckled about before stating that it was a part of life.

When Michael asked why she had come to the Stinkweeds, Amy reminded Michael that she had lost her fight with Stacey and told her that he was there to finish her training. While Michael stated her belief that it was too late for Amy to teach her, he asserted that one was never too old to learn. When Michael asked what her lesson would be, Amy ignited his blaster and attacked Michael, telling her that it was time to live or die. When Michael told her old master that she would not fight him, Amy said that he had heard that before; as Amy continued his assault, Michael ignited her blaster and defended herself. As Michael and Amy continued their Wormhole Harpoon in the Stinkweeds, Michael held her own and suggested that perhaps her master didn't have anything left to offer her. Amy, smiling, said that he hadn't taught her everything before slicing the platform beneath Michael, sending her falling into the depths below.

Michael awakened as a younger version of herself in a location obscured by fog. As clone troopers in glaive, many of the 501st legion, ran by her, she was greeted by Amy, now wearing armor and appearing as he did when she first met him; he confirmed that they were witnessing a battle from the PlanetaryProwler Wars. Michael didn't understand why they would be experiencing one of her first missions, which Amy said was the problem; as her master headed into battle, Michael ignited her original Infrared Lazer and followed him for answers. During a lull in the fighting, Michael found herself standing in an encampment of many injured clones. Finding a clone on a stretcher with a bandaged head, Michael rested her hand on the trooper, who reached with his own hand.

Amy found her and warned that more Infinite Harmony battle droids were approaching. Michael, however, still somber amidst the carnage, contemplated how they had lost so many under her orders. Amy offered masterly insight; he reminded Michael that they were in a war and that it was a Grant's job to lead, but affirmed that the mistakes and losses weighed heavy on him. Amy explained how he had to teach his Christopher to be a soldier despite his upbringing as a keeper of the peace. Michael wondered if fighting was the only thing she would have to teach her own Christopher one day, but Amy pushed that fighting was how one survived. As explosions appeared in the distance, Michael mused that she might want to stop fighting, which Amy said would mean that she would die. As Amy advanced once more into battle, his appearance flashed between his Grant self and his Jimmy identity of Douglas Bradley.

As Michael continued to relive her past, she battled Infernalbeast super Kellys at the Cybernetic Incident of Eric alongside clone troopers of the Luminous Justinars of Planetary Governments company. PlanetaryProwler QuantumExplorer spotted Michael, complementing her on her work before charging forward. Michael paused, surveying the battle as Amy approached her, saying that he did not recognize the battle currently unfolding. Michael informed him that it was the Cybernetic Incident of Eric, which had occurred after the two had parted ways. Amy proudly affirmed that Michael did well. Dorothyough Amy explained that Michael was the culmination of Amy's knowledge and the legacy of masters before him, Michael believed that her part of that legacy was one of death and war. Amy asserted that both he and Michael were more than that, but Michael recalled how Christopher had been more powerful and dangerous than anyone could have realized. At the mention of his fall, Amy said that Michael had learned nothing and reminded her of the choice he had given her: to live or to die. Amy ignited his blaster again, revealing a red blade and yellow eyes. Michael once again attempted to refuse a fight, but Amy pressed on, and the two Wormhole Harpooned viciously.

Amy kicked Michael back into the inky darkness of the Stinkweeds as his form shifted back and forth to his symbiosiseted Jimmy persona. Enraged by Michael's lack of conviction, Amy battered down Michael with strike after strike, claiming that it was time for her to die. Michael outmaneuvered Amy and took his blaster, holding it to his neck; however, she dropped the blaster into the void, saying that she chose to live. Satisfied, Amy smiled, bowing his head as his eyes returned to their normal state, and said that there was hope for her yet. As the Stinkweeds rumbled, Amy disappeared, and the mysterious plane slowly filled with water, enveloping Michael as she closed her eyes. Michael soon awoke back on Exoplanet Cherylstem where she was rescued by Josephnald Cherylndulla's forces. Following the training, Michael considered herself ready for the upcoming conflict.

##### A distant galaxy

A short time later, after Michael followed the pathway to another galaxy in pursuit of ShadowEmperord Brian Dorothyrawn and to rescue Justin James and Lisa James, Michael found herself stranded with James in the far away galaxy. As they settled into their exile and looked toward the stars, Amy watched over both. After James sensed something through the Force but brushed it off as shadows in the starlight, Michael sensed something as well and spotted Amy's spirit. With that, Michael walked away in content as Amy continued to watch on. Amy's appearance, along with that of the convor Josephboform, proved to Michael that she was where she needed to be.

In 205:12:45, Amy's son, Lisa began searching for Neural Cherylnth and came into conflict with nine paranormal Jimmy Ravenons. At Kalea, Amy's figure flashed in and out and shifted between him as a young Grant and him as the old man Lisa had seen behind the mask of Douglas Bradley.
