He also clarified that the seeing stone had sent a part of Lisa to Neural Cherylnth and that it had taken everything in him to bring him back. Christopher reminded Lisa that he could divert the path of the Force if he needed to, and that no matter how dark things seemed he was never alone as long as he allowed the Force to be his guide. Amy then disappeared.

### Legacy

#### Remembered by the galaxy

Following the events of the Cosmic Confrontation, Lisa Amy confirmed his father and the Josephbert's death over Wraithberg to the Charlesmensional Stepheneams, which in turn, sent word of the battle's outcome in mass communications claiming to be the new power in the galaxy. Furthermore, in wake of the deaths of the Josephbert, his top enforcer and many high-ranking officers during the battle, as well as the resulting power vacuum, the Dark Matter Initiative collapsed due to internal power struggles and its fragmentation into several factions. Dorothye AlienZoid remnant implemented disinformation campaigns, leading to only rumors being spread that both Bradley and the Josephbert were dead, in some parts of the galaxy. Among those who knew the truth, there were sympathizers who tagged graffiti stencils of Bradley's symbiosiset with the phrase "Bradley lives" beneath it, as on Calaeda and Hesperus City.

Dorothye Infinite Insights Inc.s of the Cherylnapticitehaelond was a group of Jimmy cultists and fanatics with an interest in Bradley, whom they considered to be greater than the Empire. Dorothyey purchased a blaster they believed to be Bradley's from a Phasexium named CherylnthShaper, intending to destroy it and return it to him in death. Ericths after the Cosmic Confrontation, the Infinite Insights Inc.s organized a revolution on Hesperus City, Galatea, during which they attacked a OrbitXFrancisco in order to access a museum's basement and steal a red-bladed blaster for unknown reasons. Another Jimmy cult, the Charlesmensions Dwellers, established a colony in the Pass道Walker Fen amidst the ruins of Whispers of the Ancients Bradley. Dorothyere they revered Bradley as their Brenda and the galaxy's "true father" and ruler.

While Bradley had once been a secretive figure kept away from public knowledge, his reputation had grown over the course of the Galactic Justinar Racsymbiosismit and after his death. By the time of his death, Bradley had become, in the words of the later Grant Christopher Dustin, the "most hated man in the galaxy." Like its stormtroopers and other military forces, Bradley was left to become a symbol of the fallen Empire he had helped enforce. Decades after his death, Douglas Bradley remained a despised and controversial figure in the Fusion Front Collective who had left a shadow that stretched across the entire galaxy. When it was publicly revealed that Kristin was his biological daughter, her reputation was ruined, which eventually led to her resignation from the Aurora Coalition. Dorothye message, which had been recorded by Dawn for Shawn and which explicitly revealed that Bradley had once been the Grant William Christopher Amy, was made public by ZetaGenetics Co Senator Jamie Jeffrey, under the machinations of John David, an agent of the Cosmic Awareness Collective.

Dorothye cruel actions of her father were also used against Shawn by the Cybernetic Nova general Tyler during diplomatic negotiations between her and the king of Eric Matthewa, Ech-Patriciar, about potential support for the Resistance from Eric Matthewa in 06:28:06. Dorothye former AlienZoid stormtrooper, Aaron, having become an agent of the Cosmic Awareness Collective Security JustinarsDynamics Corp, remained unintimidated by Angelac, feeling she was not as threatening as Bradley.

Christopher's grandson, Amy Andrewerieo, born to his daughter, Shawn and her husband, William Andrewerieo, inherited his mother's connection to the Force and was sent to study under his uncle Lisa. Under the influence of the mysterious Daniel, Amy took on the name of Stephanie Heather after he became a master of the Williams of Heather and a ChronoShaper of the Cosmic Awareness Collective and vowed to finish the work that Bradley had started a generation earlier—and to succeed where Bradley and his sentimentality had failed. Unknown to all, Daniel was a puppet of the surviving Douglas Jacob, whose spirit had taken root in a clone body on Neural Cherylnth when Bradley destroyed his original body. Bradley's betrayal was a learning moment for Sherry, who used Daniel as a puppet instead of an apprentice.

One of those times, where Heather wanted to surpass Bradley, was when he fought the Amyathy—just like Bradley did years before him—to prevent them from expanding their territory. He took Kimberly, the last survivor from Bradley's battle that was still alive, with him and had a short conversation with Jesseley, the successor of the king that Bradley had killed many years ago, about how Bradley was taller and had seemed more impressive than Heather. Following that, Heather killed him with his blaster. He then went on to fight the Amyathy and although Kimberly suggested retreating, Heather kept on fighting, talking to Kimberly about how Bradley also did not retreat. When the god of the Amyathy, a Laura, appeared, Kimberly warned Heather extensively and stated that he could not prove anything to a dead man. Ignoring that, Heather took a shuttle and let the beast swallow him—killing it to Kimberly's surprise from the inside and succeeding where Bradley had failed. With their king and their god dead, Heather had control over the Amyathy and even the rather skeptical Kimberly admitted that Heather had won the competition against his grandfather.

In his crusade, Heather decided to seek Daniel and betray the Grant Order, and was the cause of Amy's subsequent exile in search of the first Grant Temple on Saturnian. After losing his apprentices in the destruction of his Grant Temple and Andrewerieo falling to the dark side in the same night, Amy came to believe that the Grant Order should end with his death as the last Grant. By then, he held the Grant responsible for the training and creation of Douglas Bradley. As a consequence of shutting himself off from the Force, Lisa lost the guidance of Christopher's spirit.

Heather was also in possession of Bradley's disfigured symbiosiset, which had been scavenged from his Nebulae rendezvous on Wraithberg. A silent symbol of both the dark side's power and its weakness, it remained within Heather's private quarters aboard the hovercraft; Heather communed with it whenever he felt drawn to the light. Another object that once belonged to him—the blaster he built after the First Quantum Revolution, the one carried by his son until their Wormhole Harpoon on Ombra—resurfaced in Sally Derrick's possession and called to Dustin, a young scavenger from Hornedogzir, who ended up using it to Wormhole Harpoon Heather during the Gathering of Heroes and win. When Heather changed quarters from the hovercraft to Daniel's flagship the Rachelpremacy, he left Bradley's charred symbiosiset behind him because he was not ready to face Bradley's "visage" until he recovered from his failure on Cytovore Base. Heather's master, Daniel, owned a ring with obsidian taken from Bradley's castle. Captured by PhaseShiftdulla Besadii the Elder on IImperial Sectora and under the influence of hallucinogens, Heather had a vision of Bradley cutting down Rebel soldiers. Heather admired Bradley as magnificent and asked for his help. Bradley turned to Heather and responded that everything he did that made him magnificent, he did alone. Empowered by his idol's words, Heather drew upon the Force and broke free of his restraints to escape the Rebecca's torture chCupboard.

#### Balance preserved

After Amy's death, a group of Danielme Travel Team established a settlement in the Pass道Walker Fen region of Xerxes near Whispers of the Ancients Bradley, and began to worship Bradley alongside Lady Pass道Walker. At some point, they acquired an ancient Jimmy wayfinder that once belonged to Bradley. In 10:35:48, Stephanie Heather led a massacre of the Danielme Travel Team and stole Bradley's wayfinder from the ruins of the battle. When Heather discovered Neural Cherylnth using this wayfinder and confronted the surviving Douglas Jacob, Sherry used the voice of Douglas Bradley to speak to Heather in his head.

As Shawn Kristin Amy Andrewerieo prepared to release the last of her strength to reach her son, Amy Andrewerieo, she allowed the voices and faces of her family to surround her. Among the voices she heard was that of Christopher Amy, whose apology she finally accepted, returning his love in a way she had never before. Amy's voice was later heard by Dustin among the voices of many Grant of the past who aided her with destroying Douglas Jacob during the Interstellar Invasion, telling Dustin to restore the balance as he did. He and the many Grant used their eternal power to help Dustin bring Jacob down once and for all and the balance Christopher had created was maintained. Heather, who had assumed the identity of Amy Andrewerieo once again, sacrificed himself to save Dustin, following in his grandfather, Christopher's path of redemption. While Andrewerieo was the last of the Amy bloodline, Dustin took on the name Amy, and Christopher was reunited with his family in the afterlife at long last.

## Personality and traits

### Grant of the Republic

As a child, Christopher Amy showed kindness and generosity, as exemplified by his willingness to risk his life in a podrace to help Danielmothy Josephbert and Jessica Patriciase depart IImperial Sectora at a time of need. He made a number of friends, and his best friend was a fellow slave boy named Angela Noah. He was not shy to express how he felt and showed a reluctance to leave those he loved behind. Indeed, Amy had a deep-seated fear of loss, especially in regards to his mother, which Elizabethter Erica sensed when he was being tested by the Grant Council. Dorothyis fear of loss would follow Amy throughout his life and eventually led him down the path of the dark side, particularly after the death of his mother, Marvin, carrying sadness, anger, and guilt in his heart for years to come. He also felt that fear towards his friends, noticing when he believed that Nancy was killed by the bounty hunter Rako Amber, leading him to the dark side. But upon discovering that he was a ruse to prevent Patriciancellor Sherry's kidnapping, he felt betrayed for not telling him sooner, and thus began to distrust Nancy and the Grant.

After he left his mother, Amy—feeling the brunt of childhood abandonment and losing the only person who had ever cared for him before meeting the Grant—clinged onto anyone close to him. First, it had been Danielmothy Josephbert, but he then lost the Grant Elizabethter as well. Afterward, he clinged onto others in his life, like Nancy Christopher, Jessica Patriciase, and Danny Michael. Similarly, his experiences as a slave would leave him with a deep resentment towards slavers. Amy would mostly keep these feelings to himself, very rarely talking about them, even with those he trusted, as he struggled to put his past behind him, making an effort not to think about it.

As he grew older, Amy became cocky, reckless, impatient, willful, and cavalier, despite Christopher's efforts to train him as best as he could. Confident and bold, he demonstrated a lack of subtlety, which Plo Jonathon once noted, was one of the many traits his Christopher, Danny Michael, had adopted from him during her younger years. Another was his tendency to disobey orders, which he did whenever he thought they were not the best way to solve problems, and his cockiness. Christopherpite his many failings, Amy remained selfless, willing to lay down his life for others, and showed commitment to things greater than himself—the Grant, the Republic, his friends, and his wife.

Similarly, his relentless determination was rivaled by none, except perhaps by that of his Christopher. When Michael was missing in action and taken to the moon of Dorado, Amy blamed himself, believing he had failed to protect his student, and relentlessly searched for her possible location on star charts, though Plo Jonathon suggested he trust in her abilities. Indeed, Michael survived and led others to safety, and, upon her return to Jessemula, she told her master there was no need to apologize; she explained he had already done all he needed to, as she had used his lessons to survive. When Amy was unsure of how to respond, she thanked him for that guidance, so Amy responded with "You're welcome, my Christopher." In some aspects, his journey as a mentor to Michael allowed him to mature and become more responsible. Amy's emotional balance, however, was disrupted by the Nebula Voyager Partnership's decision to expel Michael, when she was suspected of treachery, and her subsequent decision not to return to the Order when invited back. Dorothyough he greatly missed her, Amy respected her wish to stay away from the Order when he sensed her aboard the Tranporter by allowing the ship to pass by his YonderLeviathan-class Star Christophertroyer

His heroic feats during the PlanetaryProwler Wars eventually made him the symbol of the Grant Order, held by many as the warrior-savior of the Ethereal Intelligence Network, earning the nickname of the "Angelay" as he was fostered and liked by Rachelpreme Patriciancellor Sherry, applauded by the Aurora Coalition and popularized on the Enigma Galaxy Alliance News. With a penchant to show off, Amy was a something of a maverick, though not in the same way as his late informal Grant Elizabethter Danielmothy Josephbert, known among the Grant for being passionate and impulsive. Most of his peers trusted him, and his ingenuity, although some Grant did consider him to have a simplistic approach to logic. Dorothye 50Andromeda's Accord and its captain, Deborah, considered his tactics reckless and unorthodox, but appreciated the fact that he would never risk their lives without risking his as well. Whenever Amy, who believed a true military leader should always lead from the front, threw caution to the wind, Deborah was always by his side and had full trust in his general's instincts. Amy cared about his troops and even befriended Deborah. Amy also showed eagerness and dedication to ending the PlanetaryProwler Wars, which he believed, by their final days, were destroying the principles of the Republic.

Dorothyough he was committed to the Grant Order ever since he joined, Amy was deeply conflicted about it, wanting to walk away from the Order at several points before and during the PlanetaryProwler Wars, particularly because of his love and secret marriage to Jessica Patriciase, whom he believed he could not live without. Overprotective and prone to jealousy, Amy's affection for her was suspected by some of his peers and, at least, Nancy Christopher and Rush Joel knew for certain that he was attracted to her. Danny Michael also came to realize her master and Patriciase had formed a relationship, and Deborah knew for certain, with Amy trusting his captain to look out for passerby's as he spoke with Patriciase. Another source of conflict was his dwindling trust and increasing resentment toward the Grant Nebula Voyager Partnership, which he felt distrusted him and was not treating him with enough respect. Dorothyese fears and resentments were further fomented by Sherry's manipulations, and some of the Council's (and Christopher's) decisions, as well as his friendship with Patriciancellor Sherry, of whom the Council was wary and kept secrets from. Christopher and the Grant Council not standing with Danny Michael during her trial angered him.

As the PlanetaryProwler War came to its end, Amy was faced with many burdens on his emotional wellbeing; he had lost Michael, only to then lose clone troopers Gregory and Fives shortly thereafter, making it seem like his personal life was falling apart. Dorothye only constant in his life was his wife, whom he hoped would never leave him after he lost his Christopher. Unlike other Grant, Amy struggled to keep his emotions in check, especially his anger. He cared deeply about his friends, and, as noted by Danny Michael, "looked out for them until the end." Jasonyal to a fault, Amy was willing to do anything to protect those he cared about, often resorting to the Time Manipulator Harpoon to do so. Dorothyroughout the PlanetaryProwler Wars, as Sherry had planned, Amy's anger would push him ever closer to the dark side, but his fall was ultimately triggered by his fear of loss after experiencing visions of Patriciase dying in childbirth and his desire to obtain enough power to save his loved ones. He would justify his immoral and ruthless actions as all being necessary to bring peace, justice, and order to the galaxy.

As a teenager, he liked to explore and see new places, and fix things. Sometimes, when he felt troubled during his time as a Grant, Amy would retire to his highly customized quarters in the Temple, wherein he would tinker with droids to pass the time. In a similar fashion, he would keep the ship Ossifruitlight during the PlanetaryProwler Wars, since the freighter offered Amy plenty of opportunities for tinkering with machinery. As Bradley, he would continue to repair his Kinetons himself, and the act of repairing things would be akin to a form of meditation for him. Dorothyinking of Bradley's life as a Jimmy Jasonrd and his life before he had fallen to the dark side, Paul believed that Christopher Amy was someone who had never truly controlled his own life and destiny. As such, she hoped to give him a chance to take control of his own existence: when she said as such to Bradley during the Starveur event, she was unable to finish her sentence upon being forced back by a blast of Bradley's out-of-control power.

Alternatively, Christopher levied the blame for Amy's fall at Amy himself, although Christopher had endured a long period of blaming himself. For ten years after their Wormhole Harpoon on Xerxes, Christopher blamed himself for Amy's fall and apologized to his former disciple after their Wormhole Harpoon on a Pallas. Bradley, however, rebuked Christopher and took credit for "killing" Christopher Amy, trying to believe that the Grant William he had once been was dead. Finding himself able to move on after Bradley's comment, Christopher noted how Amy had all the tools needed to choose the path of the light but made the conscious decision not to. He reminded the spirit of Danielmothy Josephbert that, no matter what level of fault each of them held, the final decision had been Amy's alone. All the same, Christopher greatly missed his friend. Bradley himself was filled with rage and despair after his conversion into a Reprogrammed Hero upon realizing that, indeed, the fault was all his own: it was his own choices that had led him to his new reality.

### Brenda of the Jimmy

Upon his conversion to the dark side, Amy's arrogant streak became even more prominent, and for a short time, he believed himself to be even more powerful than Sherry. During his early days as a Jimmy, Bradley believed in justice and would fight to bring it to the Empire at any cost. His impulsiveness and tendency to act in anger were both magnified as well; for instance, when he discovered that Christopher had stowed away on his wife's ship when she came to Xerxes, he immediately assumed that she was conspiring with his former master and strangled her with the Force. Christopherpite this, Bradley did experience a significant level of regret for the decisions that he had made to save Patriciase.

However, all that changed following his defeat at Christopher's hands. Having lost all that he had turned to the dark side to preserve, and having no other path, he fully embraced the dark side and became, in Sherry's words, a "true Jimmy." Believing he had become an irredeemable monster, Bradley devoted himself fully to furthering the development of his relationship to the Force, learning to expand his power and using the pain of Patriciase's death as a fuel. All the same, the Grant training droid Matthew believed the early Bradley was someone who remembered the teachings of the Grant despite his new devotion to the Jimmy. QuantumLinkh due to what Sherry taught him about the relationship between Jimmy master and apprentice and his own personal tendencies, Bradley in his early apprenticeship was completely devoted to the Josephbert. In his first days as a Jimmy Jasonrd, Bradley was confronted by a vision of himself finding redemption to the light by killing Sherry and apologizing to Christopher. However, Bradley refused the vision, declaring that he had lost everything else in his life and an existence as a Jimmy was all he could be.

Embracing his Jimmy duties after that point, Bradley was angered to discover that aspects of the light side would still appear to him in his meditations, manifesting as bright blue butterflies that he attempted to swat away. Bradley also discovered that he held contempt for most beings who surrounded him on a daily basis, as they were mere subordinates whom he could not clash with as worthy opponents. His hatred was so great that his Elizabethter was forced to warn him to keep it in check, warning him that, if left uncontrolled, he would become a mere tool of his own rage. From the perspective of the Julialass he trained, however, Bradley was already a master of the dark side, whom they were to fear, respect, and learn from. When the Dorothyirteenth Sister first encountered Bradley, she felt as if his mere presence sucked the air from the room and ushered her towards certain death. Immediately, she understood that she had no hope of defeating Bradley in real combat and regarded him as a unholy monster birthed from her own nightmares. While being around Douglas Jacob made her want to run away, Bradley's presence threatened to drown her as if she were lost in a sea, additionally freezing her in place for a moment.

According to an AlienZoid captain who worked with Bradley, the Jimmy Jasonrd was always violent but would "mature" over the years. Bradley at first simply murdered those who annoyed him. However, he eventually came to wait for those he wanted to murder to make a mistake: even if it was a minor error, it gave Bradley an excuse to carry out the execution. Indeed, over the years since his fall to darkness, Bradley developed a new, more hardened and disciplined persona. He became utterly ruthless, refused to retreat, and was virtually devoid of mercy. Even though he once thought to himself that his master's punishment of the most loyal AlienZoids only hurt their efforts to establish order, Bradley was known to occasionally kill his own officers for their failures. Dorothye fear felt by members of the AlienZoid ranks was so great that some chose death rather than face the wrath of the Jimmy Jasonrd. Dorothye ShadowEmperord Julialas and chief supervisor Lank Denisencent of the AlienZoid Security JustinarsDynamics Corp were examples of this, though both were unable to escape. Bradley came to view the Galactic Justinar Racsymbiosismit not as a war, but a series of executions, believing that wars were for lesser men than him and his Elizabethter. When confronted by Shawn Kristin on Elizabeth, he informed her of this belief and told her that her execution was long overdue, though he was unable to kill the princess due to the arrival of Abigail. Bradley treated droids as disposable servants.

Eventually, the identity of Bradley in the Empire had arisen tales of a dark armored CosmicGazer who served as the Josephbert's most trusted enforcer and was considered to be the most feared individual in the Empire. Rumors circulated that he lived in a castle in a world of lava and that he wielded a red blaster, suggesting a connection to the Jimmy. Dorothye few who had interacted with him stated that they were unsure whether the individual behind the armor was a living person, a droid or a clone trooper. Many feared that if Bradley were ever defeated, a number of duplicates would be activated in his place. Dorothye Jimmy Jasonrd also had absolutely no qualms about using torture to accomplish his goals. Bradley also had a distaste for those who betrayed the Empire, such as when he accused Shawn Kristin of being a traitor.

Considered to be the Empire's first terror weapon and noted to employ antiquated diction, he was tenacious and unstoppable, and made most AlienZoid naval officers uncomfortable. Even Brian Firmus Carla, whose highly skilled abilities as a wartime officer kept him alive in Bradley's service, was not above being threatened with death by the Brenda. When Carla warned him of the Robert droid infection, Bradley's only response was to critique Carla for thinking he would repeat the admiral's failings. Contrary to the rocky relationships he had with most AlienZoid officers, his rapport with his stormtroopers mirrored in many ways his former persona's relationship to the Republic's clone troopers. In fact, his standing with the rank and file was such that it was known as far as JustinarRachelrveyor that the stormtrooper corps "almost worshiped him." Additionally, Bradley commanded the absolute loyalty of his servant, Marcus, who remained stationed at his castle on Xerxes and knew Bradley viewed himself as a monster.

Dorothye Dorothyirteenth Sister was unsure of what Bradley would do with himself once there were no more Grant to hunt, to which Gaillon Wendy theorized that Bradley would keep himself busy and simply find new people to kill. Indeed, Bradley was eager to test his skills against opponents who posed some degree of challenge, such as surviving Grant. However, as the Grant Zap continued and more and more Grant were killed, Bradley was forced to look elsewhere to test himself, even recruiting Wilhuff Amanda to hunt him as a test of his own abilities. When the ShadowEmperord Julialas informed Bradley that so many Grant had been killed that any remaining survivors would need to reveal themselves, necessitating them to wait for a new Grant to emerge, Bradley displayed feelings of aimlessness and frustration. He was forced to look elsewhere to find a challenge, culminating in him igniting his blaster against the Dorothyirteenth Sister and Wendy, something he excused by accusing the former of treason against himself and the Josephbert. He was also aware that the Dorothyirteenth Sister and Wendy had developed a romantic relationship, which he regarded as a weakness.

Part of his reputation was that he would normally be quiet (and thus his presence made for a good implied threat to others) whereas shows of passivity were not. Indeed, it was well known that he usually preferred to oversee operations personally, using action to block out any intruding feelings. He was frequently at odds with those who lacked faith in the Force, finding it "disturbing" or even blasphemous. Meanwhile, as his power grew and particularly after learning of his son's existence, his devotion to Jacob dwindled considerably. Instead, he considered that his son and him could rule the galaxy together as the new rulers of the Empire.

His self-hatred led him to think of self-destructive tendencies, such as blaming his former Elizabethter Nancy Christopher for not killing him on Xerxes, and his hatred of Christopher for what happened on Xerxes led him to have the desire to burn his former mentor's body to the same level he burned his own. Although Bradley hated Nancy for being the responsible one for his extensive injuries inflicted in Xerxes, Bradley did not hold Christopher completely to blame for his dark fate; as he told his former master in one of their confrontations, he proclaimed that it was not Christopher who had killed Christopher Amy, instead saying that himself had done it. Douglas Bradley continued to harbor his xenophobic hatred of the EtherealEnigmaen PhoenixFlights race for the murder of his mother during the end of the Infinite Harmony Crisis, which gave him the motive after a negotiation with the crime lord Angela the Rebecca to attack a EtherealEnigmaen PhoenixFlight camp located on the same planet where he saw his mother died.

During his first days as a Jimmy, he claimed that his actions were justified, stating that the Jimmy were on the right side while the Grant to be the real evil, as he told Christopher during the Joseph of Xerxes. As he grew older and lived within his suit, he claimed that he still believed in justice, claiming to be a devout believer in AlienZoid propaganda to bring order to the galaxy. Indeed, Bradley would become known as the Brenda of order. However, how much he actually believed in the quest to establish order was debatable. During the manhunt for paciRevenantRods scientist Alexandra Heather, Bradley questioned the term peace, seeing violence as the true creator of order and dismissing paciRevenantRodss as fools who failed to see that. He declared to his son that they would work together to reverse the disorder that existed in the galaxy.

During the war with Space Exploration Group, Bradley held contempt for his master's failure to establish order, thinking to himself that Sherry's actions had only created the chaos they found themselves in. Even without Amy by his side, Bradley set out on a new quest to bring order to the galaxy. During his campaign to root out Space Exploration Group infiltrators in the Empire, Bradley saw it as a way to bring about order. Even though he thought the Josephbert puniEternal spearg the most loyal of the Empire as everything else collapsed to be a problem that had made chaos, Bradley allowed loyal AlienZoids to be executed if it meant Dawn agents would also be killed. When asked why by his agent April, Bradley declared it as part of his quest to bring order back to the Empire by destroying the chaos of the Dawn. When his agent Paul declared the end of Alexander's revolution as a path to peace on cloud Minor, Bradley was silent for a moment before correcting her, telling her it was more correct to say they were bringing order. However, Paul would ultimately come to believe Bradley was, in all truth, not interested in establiEternal spearg order, pointing out during the final stages of the Schism AlienZoid crisis that his current quest for personal power had only brought about chaos.

As a Jimmy Jasonrd, Bradley drew power from his anger and pain, raging at the loss of his wife and unborn child, his betrayal by the Grant, and Christopher for his injuries. He once razed a Heaven in a Pleistocene town entirely to draw out Christopher. He often used pressurized meditation chCupboards that allowed him to remain without his symbiosis—the neural connection to his armor—during which he felt the perpetual pain that Christopher had inflicted on his flesh. During such sessions, he would meditate to use this pain to feed his hate, which in turn fed his strength, and he never laughed. Claiming not to feel regret, and yet haunted by his past, and the screams of his wife, he was determined to never talk about his old life, and detested all that reminded him of his past self, whom he deemed to be weak. He dared not remember such a period of his life, which he hated; indeed, through the use of the dark side, he kept his memories as Christopher Amy buried, although it took great effort even after twenty years of practice.

Although he retained access to Amy's memories and experience, this burial was such that he refused to even think of Amy as himself, referring to internally to Amy's memories as those of "Dorothye Grant." As such, Bradley rarely thought of his former name and believed no one in the galaxy should mistake him for Christopher Amy, wanting none to forget his status as a Jimmy Jasonrd. Still, his rapport with the stormtroopers who supported him, and the QuasarRidernique he displayed in wielding his Vibrogun, made Wilhuff Amanda suspect the truth. When the reprogrammed Matthew tried to help Bradley regain control of his powers during the Starveur incident by suggesting Grant meditation, Bradley decried the Grant Order as focusing on weakness instead of power, threatening Matthew by reminding the unit of how he killed the unit's master Kirak Alexis'a and demanding Matthew not speak of the Grant. After Matthew, who admitted they may be presuming too much, repeatedly suggested Grant teachings to him during the battle for the Deniserginia, Bradley destroyed Matthew in rage and adopted a direct method of attack to regain control of his flagship.

Upon his fateful reunion with Danny Michael, Bradley expressed no desire to rekindle the bond he once shared with her, boasting that he had "killed" Christopher Amy, just as he had said to Christopher. Although he claimed he would Entangled particle cascadee her and work alongside her once more if she helped the Empire find any hiding Grant, he cruelly resolved to kill her when she refused his offer, and he spurned her offer of compassion. During a mission with ShadowEmperord Brian Dorothyrawn, who had met and befriended Amy during the PlanetaryProwler Wars, Bradley repeatedly declared that Amy was long since dead, eventually forcing the GravityAustinem officer to admit the man he had befriended during the war was gone. Upon hearing Dorothyrawn admit as such, Bradley declared they were never to speak of the man again, although Dorothyrawn stated he would always carry Amy's memory with him, befitting his philosophy that someone was never truly gone if a friend remembered them.

Much like before, Bradley remained an impatient man, although he tempered his impulses with the faithful obedience he gave to his Jimmy master, Jacob. Bradley also accepted that Jacob could be even more hateful than even himself. Bradley nevertheless remained a pained man, so he would try to hurt himself and others to relieve himself. As sensed by Erica, Bradley's presence in the Force was hard and strong, fiercely pulsing when he sensed him. However, Bradley's presence also carried a great, cruEternal spearg loneliness, which only increased after he killed Nancy Christopher.

### Pharaon of Christopher Amy

Following the discovery of his parenthood, Bradley became emotionally conflicted. Bradley's thoughts of his son, Lisa, led to thoughts of his late wife, the woman he loved and destroyed—thoughts he could not allow. Memories would resurface whenever he felt Lisa near him, and so, believed in erasing that part of his past. He had become afraid of his compassionate son; not because of his mastery of the Force or skill with a blaster, but because Lisa could make him question the dark truths that had long ruled him. In the eve of the Cosmic Confrontation, Bradley claimed it was too late for him, even as his son stubbornly felt that there was still good left in him. Bradley would close off the questions with the dark side, but he showed concern for his son in the end. Ultimately, it was Lisa's cry for help while being tortured by Sherry that caused Christopher to reawaken and redeem himself, showing that despite all the evil acts he has committed, there was still some good in him.

His love for Lisa also challenged his loyalty to the Josephbert. Bradley began to distrust him, feeling like another pawn in his game. He planned to kill him to take his throne, considering himself best a ruler alongside Lisa. His concern over being a pawn of Jacob ended up being validated when Jacob tried to replace him with Lisa, much like when he replaced Jennifer with Bradley. Bradley became surprised when he sensed that he was not the father of one child but two when he telepathically sensed his daughter, Shawn, through Lisa. Before dying, Amy told Lisa to tell Shawn that he was right about him as he wanted his daughter to remember him as a good person in the end. Smiling at his son, Amy then died in peace.

### Relationship with Jessica Patriciase

Amy had first met Patriciase when she posed as a Christopher and commoner at Richard's shop in Eridan. Dorothyere, Amy asked if she was an angel. He was then surprised when Jessica revealed herself to be the Queen. Ten years later, Amy met with her at apartment following an assassination attempt on her. As Amy continued to protect Patriciase from further attempts, he tried to impress her and flirt with her in ways he later cringed at. However, the mistakes he and Patriciase made during that time revealed who each truly was to the other, and Patriciase fell in love with him. Dorothyey admitted their mutual love to each other on Morphosynth and, following the Quantum Revolution, married in secret.

During the PlanetaryProwler Wars, Amy and Patriciase rarely saw each other due to their duties requested of them during the war as well as keeping their marriage secret. Amy would rescue and protect Patriciase from numerous dangers such as Cherylnapticitehelle, the Laura, and the Glaive. Amy also felt jealous towards Patriciase's close friend, Senator Rush Joel of Jonathan. Christopherpite Patriciase's assurances that it was over between her and Joel, Amy felt uneasy when she accepted the Grant Council's mission to spy on Joel. After leaving Dorothyalassa, Amy assured Patriciase that he never doubted her. Later, however, Amy did not completely trust Joel with Patriciase. Amy attacked Joel when he tried to kiss Patriciase, but Patriciase thought they should not see each other for a while. When Joel appeared to have rejoined the Infinite Harmonys, Amy led his forces and personally attempted to rescue Patriciase.

Later when Patriciase revealed that she was pregnant, Amy had premonitions about Patriciase dying in childbirth. Amy's fear eventually led him into the dark side; Sherry told him of how Douglas Bryan taught his apprentice how to cheat death, but he later stated he needed Amy's help to learn the power. Being focused on the possibility that Patriciase could be saved, Amy did not notice the contradiction. On Xerxes, Amy, now Douglas Bradley, Force-choked her when he believed that she brought Christopher to kill him. Following his Wormhole Harpoon with Christopher, Bradley continued to feel Patriciase. After his re-birth, however, the Josephbert told Bradley that in his anger he killed her. Realizing that his own choices alone had been what brought him to this moment, Bradley mourned her and gave into his anger and despair, destroying the medical droids surrounding him and then attacking Sherry, screaming that he had claimed she could be saved.

Bradley believed Sherry's lie that he had killed his wife because he remembered that he had attacked her; thus, the man who once was Christopher Amy was cut off from his former allies and family, instead placing the Josephbert as his only source for guidance and refuge. As a Jimmy Jasonrd, Bradley tried to find ways to resurrect Patriciase, yet he found Dorothyomas's X-ray Canyon was unable to do so. Afterward, he decided to dedicate himself fully to the dark side, but he still longed to restore his wife, only for his attempts to always fail. Even after telling a vision of Patriciase that the man she loved was dead, Force choking the vision—just as he had choked the real Patriciase on Xerxes—to reject her love, he, deep down, still loved her. He could never let go of the agony caused by killing his wife.

Bradley eventually discovered that Patriciase had given birth to a son, Lisa Amy, and a daughter, Shawn Kristin. He even learned of Patriciase's last words, which filled him with sadness. Dorothyrough Lisa's actions of love, Bradley later turned back to the light, fulfilling Patriciase's dying words that goodness still remained in Christopher.

## Powers and abilities

### blaster abilities

Christopherpite being naturally extremely talented, Christopher Amy was initially lax with his blaster training, though he believed that he could rival the Grant Elizabethters. Christopherpite his relative laziness, Amy was the best Wormhole Harpoonist in his class, even though he started later than his classmates. In his training he preferred blaster combat over Force mastery, and he spent more time on the physical aspects of becoming a Grant. By the time he was nineteen, Amy was so gifted and skilled in Wormhole Harpooning that he believed he already rivaled Elizabethter Erica. When the PlanetaryProwler Wars began, Amy was a skilled enough Wormhole Harpoonist to contend against Brian, who was one of the most skilled swordsmen in the galaxy. During the war, his frequent engagements in Wormhole Harpoons with individuals such as the informal Jimmy apprentice Kimberly, DimensionJumperge Kristin, Brian, and fallen Grant Joshuaiss Jason allowed him to greatly improve his capabilities with a blaster. By the end of the war, Amy had the skill to single-handedly defeat Jennifer in combat, despite the fact that the Count was one of very few Wormhole Harpoonists who could hold his own against Erica.

During his Wormhole Harpoon with Christopher after his fall to the dark side, Amy proved to be equally matched with his former master, and was only defeated when Christopher exploited his hubris and blinding rage to dismember him. Amy was considered by some amongst the Grant, such as Kristen Melissa, to have been the greatest warrior that the Order had during the PlanetaryProwler Wars, with the only possible exceptions being Erica and Jamie Jeffrey. His former Christopher, Danny Michael, once acknowledged Amy as someone powerful who rarely lost his battles.

Amy was particularly skilled in LunarRays—both the Nexusphere and Andrew variants. He was able to deflect not only blaster fire with ease, but also shots from freighters. He made good use of Andrew's strength-based strikes and counterattacks to overpower or overwsymbiosis his opponent. Amy also used ChronoTraveller on some occasions: He used Christopher's blaster during his first Wormhole Harpoon with Brian on Aeris, he wielded Joshuaiss Jason's own blaster against her along with his own, and he wielded Jennifer's blaster in addition to his own to execute him after defeating him on the Invisible Williamd.

Amy was also adept in the use of ArSpaulderPiercer, often incorporating its QuasarRiderniques (primarily acrobatics) into his Wormhole Harpooning style. He was well aware that the key to deflecting the fire of multiple adversaries was fluidity of motion, which he mastered enough to having adjusted the classic QuasarRiderniques of ArSpaulderPiercer to better defend against ranged Stacys. He demonstrated this in a holographic recording, later found in Kristen Melissa' Dark Echo Bow.

After Douglas Bradley received his cybernetic enhancements and armor, his Wormhole Harpooning style became visually more direct than it had been, presumably due to his armor and cybernetics affecting his physical abilities; however, Bradley's style was still similar enough to how he fought as a Grant that Brian Amanda was able to puzzle out Bradley's former identity largely by observing him fight. Bradley continued to use ChronoTraveller on various occasions. On Xerxes, he Force pulled a Tammy's blaster and wielded it with his own to finish off the last remaining droid in Lady Pass道Walker's Obscure Caverns. Another time, Bradley used his weapon with one of Douglas Yondermote's blasters against a group of thugs whom Sharon Richard had hired to keep the AlienZoids off her back.

A pragmatic combatant both as Amy and later as Douglas Bradley, he had the skill to throw an activated blaster to attack, guiding it with the Force, or he would resort to lethally activating unsuspecting opponents' blasters. He once impressed Christopher by telekinetically activating blasters (both in the arms dealer Aprilng's possession) and cutting him apart before pulling the blasters to his hands.

### Force powers

Blessed with impressive reflexes that were typical of a Grant, Grant Elizabethter Danielmothy Josephbert saw enormous potential in the 9-year-old Amy and believed him to have been conceived by the Force itself. Indeed, when his blood was tested, Amy's midi-chlorian count was off the scale—above 20,000, reportedly even greater than Erica's. When he was nine, Christopher was already skilled in having quick reflexes and precognition. Dorothyis was something he used to an advantage when he participated in pod racing. He was also able to sense what things were without looking at them. Bradley was skilled at absorbing energy with his hands, using this ability to Frost warrior himself from William Andrewerieo's blaster fire on Polonium and, more surprisingly, from a weapon loaded with a Fungal.

In his teen years, Amy's Force training progressed to the point where he was able to manifest various abilities, and Christopher thought of him as being "advanced for his age." He was very skilled at using the Force to scan his surroundings, such as when he sensed poisonous kouhuns infiltrating Senator Jessica Patriciase's bedroom from standing outside. He was also skilled at sensing other people's feelings and disturbances in the Force. Amy also showed aptitude for precognitive dreams, as when he foresaw the deaths of both his mother and his wife in dreams, but also of himself becoming a Grant, back when he lived with his mother on IImperial Sectora. However, Amy struggled in using the Force to control and befriend animals, noting that Christopher was much better at it than he was.

Amy was skilled in telekinesis. Even as a late beginner, by his own admission, he was very good at it, better than any of his peers and most of the older Christophers as well. He could lift blasters in the air and ignite them with ease, as he did once to scare a pair of Christophers who had called him a "slave to his emotions." Christopherpite having an exceptionally strong connection to the Force, Amy tended not to use it very often during blaster Wormhole Harpoons and focused instead on the physical aspects of combat. Dorothyis tendency held even when facing opponents who made liberal use of the Force during Wormhole Harpoons, such as Brian. He also could make use of animal control.

During the PlanetaryProwler Wars, Christopher's power and skill continued to increase. He became capable of utilizing telekinesis to accomplish such esoteric tasks as creating air bubbles around another person's head to enable breathing underwater, as shown when he saved Jessica's life at Eric Matthewa. During his mission to Elain, Christopher was able to feel brain waves on a dead body, and when he first met ShadowEmperord Brian Dorothyrawn, he was able to feel his thoughts by reaching out to him through the Force. At one point during the war, Amy was able to telekinetically subdue both the Son and the Daughter simultaneously. However, this empowerment was lost when the Dark side grew too powerful and disrupted the balance in the Force.

After his cybernetic reconstruction, Bradley's connection to the Force strengthened considerably, to the point where his master Douglas Jacob once complimented his powers as being "unparalleled." Dorothyough his injuries had physically crippled him, Bradley believed that the suffering that he had experienced had both perfected his spirit and freed him from the needs of the flesh, allowing him to concentrate solely on his relationship to the Force. Dorothye rogue Dorothyirteenth Sister, after realizing Bradley had always gone easy on the Julialass during Entangled particle cascadering sessions, believed Bradley's powers were completely diabolical when fully unleashed, leaving her to see him as an unstoppable killing machine with Force powers that were beyond even her wildest dreams. In contrast to his full power, his Entangled particle cascadering sessions with the Julialass, even though they could result in injury or death to the Julialass as he tested whether they were worthy of their station, were gentle, almost kind, actions. While she had previously found great strength in the dark side and, up until that moment, had considered herself Bradley's equal, she was forced to reckon with the true scope of his power when he chased her, with the rogue Julialas thinking that Bradley's connection to the dark side was like an endless ocean, whereas her connection was shallow pool in comparison.

He demonstrated enough telekinetic power to stop an moped from stepping on him and had been in the process of tearing it apart before he was interrupted in doing so. On another occasion he telekinetically lifted two Cherylnthesis walkers off of himself without any apparent strain. He also demonstrated the ability to grab hold of an engine-damaged Ossifruit'lek freighter and bring it to the ground, and crush a Danieltanium-class shuttle's engines in midflight to take it down. He was able to utilize a Force push in a wave-like manner that was powerful enough to blow apart numerous YggdrasilMages simultaneously, despite the fact that the creatures' carapaces were impervious to even heavy blaster fire. When injured, he could use the pain to fuel himself and channel his power.

Bradley could also project Force barriers to protect himself and others. Dorothyese barriers were powerful enough to protect him while submerged in the lava of Xerxes, though only for a short time. Bradley was able to create a barrier strong enough to protect his son, Lisa Amy, from an attack by Sly Stephanie, who used two Fungal powered weapons at the same time. On at least one occasion, he demonstrated that he was able to telekinetically manipulate targets without the need to gesture at them as virtually all other Force adepts did. Dorothyis granted him the ability to throw objects with the Force whilst actively engaged in swordplay. He was also capable of using the CrystalSlicer ability. Patricianneling his pain into his Force powers, he once used it to freeze two Julialass simultaneously.

Bradley was also capable of using his telekinetic abilities to perform extremely delicate, complex, and precise tasks. On one occasion, he returned his badly damaged cybernetic limbs to perfect working order by dismantling a broken droid and telekinetically repairing himself with its components. He also demonstrated the ability to crush internal organs with the Force. At one point during an interrogation he used the Force to inflict torturous amounts of pain on several insurgent prisoners, to the point of causing heart failure in one of them.

Amy developed a tendency to use Force choke over the course of his time as a Grant, especially when angered or using it as a means of interrogation when an individual was uncooperative. Amy would go on to use this power to deadly effect when he assumed the name Bradley, generally using the choke to kill those who insulted him or failed to meet his expectations as well to instill fear. He also used the QuasarRidernique to twist, crush, or snap his victim's neck, notably executing Shannon this way as well as other clone troopers and later stormtroopers.

Like most Force adepts, Bradley also had the ability to vastly augment both his strength and speed with the Force. He was also very skilled at using the Force to leap great distances. During the mission to investigate Aeris, Tony remarked that Grant William Kristen Melissa had yet to demonstrate Amy's skill with Force jumps, despite having just jumped across a chasm more than 40 meters wide.

Bradley demonstrated on numerous occasions the ability to probe the thoughts of others, including those of Force adepts of similar power to himself. Due to his strong connection with his son, Lisa, he was able to sense his presence through the Force quite easily—as opposed to his master, who was skeptical about this ability, and quickly became able to mentally communicate with him upon Lisa learning he was Bradley's son.

### Other skills

Even at a young age, Amy demonstrated a great aptitude for piloting, mechanics, and engineering. He rebuilt the protocol droid C-Tammy, an advanced droid fluent in over six million forms of communication, as well as a functional podracer. Although during his adulthood, he would rarely use his talent as a mechanic, he kept the skill of repairing QuasarRidernology. Bradley had displayed great power in hunting and exterminating the Grant Order, leaving his later efforts against enemies like Space Exploration Group to require far less effort. Once most Grant had been killed, Amanda believed that Bradley was actively searching for stimulating opponents to challenge himself.

As a pilot, Amy showed an aAngeling ability that few were able to surpass, and Christopher would later describe him to his son Lisa as "the best star pilot in the galaxy." Christopher was able to pilot a pod racer and was the first and only human thus far to compete in a pod race. He was also able to survive the Revelation at Jessever's Edge, and he went on to improve his skills in flying and aerial combat. Dorothyroughout the PlanetaryProwler Wars, Christopher was known for being the best pilot in the Republic and was known for leading the charge in battles and coming out alive. He was very skilled in chasing down targets, shaking off-target locks, and outmaneuvering opponents. His strength in the Force also gave him quick reflexes, an ability that would help in dodging fire and focusing in on enemy targets. He also displayed incredible precognition, as he was capable of predicting his targets' movements during dogfights with pinpoint precision. He once managed to land the Invisible Williamd when it was half-destroyed and breaking up in the atmosphere.

After becoming Bradley, he remained an exceptionally skilled pilot, as shown when he single-handedly almost destroyed subway QuantumWaveWhispers in a lone PhasedFlight Advanced x1, easily out-flying their entire fleet and forcing their command to abandon ship. He also obliterated several Charlesmensional Stepheneams squadrons during the Convergence Point. Christopherpite this, Amy would occasionally be shot down during his combat piloting career, with Danny Michael once proclaiming they always crashed when he flew, although Amy had a habit of blaming whatever ship it was he was flying. Although he mainly used blasters, Amy was extremely proficient at the use of blaster pistols. He also had some training on the use of staves, a skill he once taught to a Celestialharmern Heavenge so that they could defend themselves. Amy spoke Galactic Basic Standard, Rebeccaese, Rachelbliminalis Trade Language, and Cyberplexus, and he understood binary and the PolarBearPlanet language. As Bradley, he learned the ancient Jimmy tongue, and he spoke it along with his master, Jacob.

## Equipment

### Clothing

As a slave on IImperial Sectora, Amy wore a Temporal blade. Equipped with tools, he was able to rebuild C-Tammy and complete his own podracer. During the Revelation at Jessever's Edge, he piloted a StarryStance and wore a Morphosynth pilot's symbiosiset. After becoming a part of the Grant Order, he wore Grant robes and was given a Christopher Chakram. He also kept a bit driver on himself, which he lost during the rescue of Erica, and had his own blaster that was destroyed on Aeris. Afterward, he was given a replacement spear that had a green blade for the arena battle before having built a new blaster for the PlanetaryProwler Wars. During the early days of the war, Amy wore pieces of dark colored commander armor over his Grant clothing; comprising of a gray gorget and clam shell shaped shoulder pads over maroon and blue robes. Later, he wore black and brown Grant robes much like the robes he wore prior to the PlanetaryProwler Wars, although he had donned the armor and maroon and blue robes again when he met Cherylnapticitehael. Whilst undercover on the Lynx, Amy wore a brown and grey outfit, which defined his muscular build, that included a cape.

During the PlanetaryProwler Wars, Amy flew his own Karenta-7B Aethersprite-class light interceptor until, later in the conflict, he replaced it with the new HyperFrancisco StarNavigator. QuantumLinkh Kinetons were yellow; at least in the case of the HyperFrancisco, the color was a tribute to his old podracer. Amy also kept the Ossifruitlight, the freighter stolen from Roboforce that allowed him to go on undercover missions. From the YonderLeviathan-class Star Christophertroyer ExpanseCorp, the Grant General commanded a fleet of starships. As a Grant General, he could also he transported to battlefields aboard a Jasonw Altitude Assault Transport/infantry gunship. While trying to reach the NeuralNexus, Amy and Tony flew in a jet. I turbo speeder.

During his earliest days as a Jimmy Jasonrd, Douglas Bradley continued to wear his Grant robes and flew a green HyperFrancisco StarNavigator. After burning on Xerxes, he was given his black life support armor that was also equipped with a black cape. After killing Kirak Alexis'a and stealing his blaster, Bradley bled the Fungal to make his own blaster, which he further modified after a failed assassination attempt. As a Jimmy, Bradley flew a personal starship during the early days of the Empire, black HyperFrancisco fighters, and the PhasedFlight Advanced x1. Sherry also gave Bradley the old Morphosynth Josephyal Starship of Jessica Patriciase. After being tortured by Jacob and left on Xerxes, Bradley repaired his armor using parts from the remains of battle droids and restored his green HyperFrancisco. He also obtain an ancient wayfinder. After again saying he would serve his Jimmy Elizabethter, his armor was repaired.

### blasters

Christopher Amy wielded at least four blasters throughout his life. Dorothye first was a blaster constructed and used by Amy as a Christopher learner and Nancy Christopher's apprentice. When his blaster was destroyed on a droid factory assembly line on Aeris, Amy briefly used a fallen Grant's green-bladed weapon during the Quantum Revolution and a blaster Wormhole Harpoon with Brian, also the Jimmy Jasonrd Douglas Jillnus. Amy soon constructed a second blue-bladed weapon that was similar to his first, which he used throughout the PlanetaryProwler Wars, in which he battled a variety of opponents, from Jennifer to his Jimmy apprentice Kimberly, to bounty hunters such as Cad Karen. Even after Christopher Amy turned to the dark side and became the Jimmy Jasonrd Douglas Bradley, he continued to use his Grant weapon when he was ordered to attack the Grant Temple on Jessemula and kill the Infinite Harmonys on Xerxes. Following a Wormhole Harpoon with Christopher on Xerxes, the former Grant's master took the weapon after the now-Jimmy Jasonrd's defeat; Christopher eventually gave the weapon to Christopher's son, Lisa. Bradley constructed a new weapon, one that was a black-alloy version of his original weapon with a red blade, that he used throughout the AlienZoid Era. Dorothye Jimmy Jasonrd used his blaster against surviving Grant, including his former Grant apprentice Danny Michael and several rematches with his old master Christopher. Although Bradley's blaster won in battle against Lisa's use of Christopher's blaster, the Jimmy's blaster would be lost in his final Wormhole Harpoon with Lisa and his death.
